印度尼西亚泗水每月 DHF 病例的负二项式 tv-DINGARCH 模型(2015-2025 年)

Negative Binomial tv-DINGARCH Models for Monthly DHF Cases in Surabaya, Indonesia (2015-2025)
作者： Aminatuz Zahro, A’yunin Sofro
摘要中文翻译流行病学研究中经常会遇到计数时间序列数据,例如登革出血热 (DHF) 病例的月度报告。INGARCH 模型已广泛用于处理此类数据中的时间依赖性和过度分散。然而,标准 INGARCH 模型假设离散程度恒定,而实际上,由于季节性或病媒控制政策等流行病学因素,离散程度可能随时间变化。准确捕捉这种随时间变化的变异性对于可靠的疾病监测至关重要。本研究将 tv-DINGARCH 模型应用于泗水 2015 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月的 DHF 病例。使用负二项分布的 tv-DINGARCH(1,1,1,1) 模型是因为它允许离散参数 \(\phi_t\) 动态演化。参数估计是使用 RStudio 中实现的条件最大似然估计方法进行的。结果表明,tv-DINGARCH 模型的 AIC = 968.43 和 BIC = 985.68,均小于标准 INGARCH 模型的结果(AIC = 1012.18,BIC = 1023.68)。似然比检验产生 LR 统计量为 47.7560,p 值小于 0.000001,强烈拒绝恒定离散。平稳性条件满足 \(\beta_2 + \alpha_2 + \beta_1 + \alpha_1 = 0.9990 1\)。从公共卫生角度来看,这些发现表明 tv-DINGARCH 模型更好地捕捉 DHF 传播的波动动态,支持更有效的监测和预警系统。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
INGARCH 模型已广泛用于处理此类数据中的时间依赖性和过度分散。
设计与对象
流行病学研究中经常会遇到计数时间序列数据,例如登革出血热 (DHF) 病例的月度报告。
核心方法
本研究将 tv-DINGARCH 模型应用于泗水 2015 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间的 DHF 病例。
主要结果
结果表明,tv-DINGARCH 模型的 AIC = 968.43 和 BIC = 985.68,均小于标准 INGARCH 模型的结果(AIC = 1012.18,BIC = 1023.68)。
解释与创新
似然比检验产生 LR 统计量为 47.7560,p 值小于 0.000001,强烈拒绝恒定离散。
科研与公卫意义
从公共卫生角度来看,这些发现表明 tv-DINGARCH 模型更好地捕捉 DHF 传播的波动动态,支持更有效的监测和预警系统。
局限与证据强度
平稳性条件满足 \(\beta_2 + \alpha_2 + \beta_1 + \alpha_1 = 0.9990 1\)。

Negative Binomial tv-DINGARCH Models for Monthly DHF Cases in Surabaya, Indonesia (2015-2025)

Authors: Aminatuz Zahro, A’yunin Sofro
Original Abstract
Count time series data are commonly encountered in epidemiological studies, such as monthly reports of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) cases. The INGARCH model has been widely used to handle temporal dependence and overdispersion in such data. However, the standard INGARCH model assumes constant dispersion, whereas in practice the dispersion level may vary over time due to epidemiological factors like seasonality or vector control policies. Accurately capturing this time-varying variability is essential for reliable disease surveillance. This study applies the tv-DINGARCH model to DHF cases in Surabaya from January 2015 to December 2025. The tv-DINGARCH(1,1,1,1) model with Negative Binomial distribution is used because it allows the dispersion parameter \(\phi_t\) to evolve dynamically. Parameter estimation is conducted using the Conditional Maximum Likelihood Estimation method implemented in RStudio. The results show that the tv-DINGARCH model yields AIC = 968.43 and BIC = 985.68, both smaller than those of the standard INGARCH model (AIC = 1012.18, BIC = 1023.68). The likelihood ratio test produces an LR statistic of 47.7560 with a p-value less than 0.000001, strongly rejecting constant dispersion. The stationarity condition is satisfied with \(\beta_2 + \alpha_2 + \beta_1 + \alpha_1 = 0.9990 1\). From a public health perspective, these findings indicate that the tv-DINGARCH model better captures the fluctuating dynamics of DHF transmission, supporting more effective surveillance and early warning systems.

通过分子模拟、引导分子动力学和伞采样对木瓜黄酮作为登革热病毒 NS5 RdRp 抑制剂进行基于结构的自由能评估

Structure-based Free Energy Evaluation of Papaya Flavonoids as Dengue Virus NS5 RdRp Inhibitors via Molecular Simulations, Steered Molecular Dynamics, and Umbrella Sampling
作者： K. L. Joseph Libin, K. S. Sindhu, J. Kavitha, S. Ramakrishnan, Ignatious Abraham, T. G. Abi
摘要中文翻译登革热病毒 (DENV) NS5 的 RdRp 结构域是一种保守且功能必需的酶,使其成为有趣的抗病毒靶点。本研究利用基于结构的计算机方法检验了番木瓜类黄酮抑制 DENV-RdRp 变构结合域的功效。使用分子动力学模拟、主成分分析和自由能图谱评估了八种黄酮类化合物的 RdRp 酶结合亲和力。分子模拟研究预测了生物系统中稳定的蛋白质-配体复合结构的形成。使用伞式采样的 SMD 模拟对进一步的蛋白质-配体结构解离和解离能量进行了量化。通过推导自由能曲线,所有八种 RdRp-类黄酮复合物均显示出与新橙皮苷、芒哈斯林和异槲皮素复合物进行蛋白质-配体结合的热力学可行性,其结合能分别为−17.35 kcal·mol[式:见文本]、-16.52 kcal·mol[式:见文本]和-16.04 kcal·mol[式:见文本]。这些结果与 MD 和 MD 后 MMGBSA 结合自由能计算一致。因此,我们使用先进的分子模拟方法成功鉴定和分析了以 DENV-RdRp 为目标的基于木瓜类黄酮的天然支架。

Structure-based Free Energy Evaluation of Papaya Flavonoids as Dengue Virus NS5 RdRp Inhibitors via Molecular Simulations, Steered Molecular Dynamics, and Umbrella Sampling

Authors: K. L. Joseph Libin, K. S. Sindhu, J. Kavitha, S. Ramakrishnan, Ignatious Abraham, T. G. Abi
Original Abstract
Dengue virus (DENV) NS5’s RdRp domain is a conserved and functionally necessary enzyme, making it an interesting antiviral target. This study examined Carica papaya flavonoids’ efficacy in inhibiting DENV-RdRp’s allosteric binding domain utilizing structure-based in silico methods. Eight flavonoids were evaluated for RdRp enzyme binding affinity using molecular dynamics simulations, principal component analysis, and free energy landscapes. The molecular simulation studies predicted the formation of stable protein–ligand complex structures in a biological system. Further protein–ligand structure dissociation and unbinding energetics were quantified using SMD simulations with umbrella sampling. By deriving free energy profiles, all eight RdRp-flavonoid complexes showed thermodynamic feasibility for protein–ligand binding with neohesperidin, manghaslin, and isoquercetin complexes having binding energies of −17.35 kcal⋅mol[Formula: see text], −16.52 kcal⋅mol[Formula: see text], and −16.04 kcal⋅mol[Formula: see text], respectively. These results were aligned with the MD and post-MD MMGBSA binding free energy calculations. Thus, we successfully identified and analyzed papaya flavonoid-based natural scaffolds that target DENV-RdRp using advanced molecular simulation methods.

2′-脱氧-2′-β-氟-4′-叠氮胞苷或阿兹夫定抑制病毒聚合酶的机制和谱

Mechanism and spectrum of inhibition of viral polymerases by 2′-deoxy-2′-β-fluoro-4′-azidocytidine or azvudine
作者： Mark R Denison, Timothy P Sheahan, Meghan V Diefenbacher, Emma Woolner, Laura J Stevens, Tia M Hughes, Hery W Lee, Egor P Tchesnokov, David C Schultz, Sara Cherry
摘要中文翻译抗病毒核苷类似物的治疗价值在 2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19) 大流行期间得到强调,瑞德西韦和莫努匹拉韦因其广谱抗病毒活性而被重新利用。胞苷类似物阿兹夫定 (FNC) 最近作为治疗人类免疫缺陷病毒 1 型 (HIV-1) 和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (SARS-CoV-2) 感染的潜在疗法而受到关注。考虑到 HIV-1 逆转录酶 (RT) 和 SARS-CoV-2 RNA 依赖性 RNA 聚合酶 (RdRp) 不同的底物特异性,统一的抑制机制仍然难以捉摸。在这里,我们评估了 FNC 的活性三磷酸盐形式 FNC-TP 对几种病毒聚合酶的抑制作用。FNC-TP掺入的相对效率遵循以下顺序:HIV-1 RT>丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)RdRp>呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)RdRp>登革热病毒2型(DENV-2)RdRp≫SARS-CoV-2 RdRp。它的掺入导致所有测试的聚合酶链终止。针对 HIV-1 的抗病毒活性先前已得到证实,此处显示了 DENV-2 的抗病毒活性。总的来说,数据表明,FNC-TP 对病毒聚合酶的抑制可以转化为针对逆转录病毒和 RNA 病毒的抗病毒活性,但对于 SARS-CoV-2,这种联系并不明显。FNC-TP 是 SARS-CoV-2 RdRp 的较差底物,并且 FNC 在细胞培养物中缺乏针对 SARS-CoV-2 的显着抗病毒活性。

Mechanism and spectrum of inhibition of viral polymerases by 2′-deoxy-2′-β-fluoro-4′-azidocytidine or azvudine

Authors: Mark R Denison, Timothy P Sheahan, Meghan V Diefenbacher, Emma Woolner, Laura J Stevens, Tia M Hughes, Hery W Lee, Egor P Tchesnokov, David C Schultz, Sara Cherry
Original Abstract
The therapeutic value of antiviral nucleoside analogs was highlighted during the coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) pandemic, with remdesivir and molnupiravir repurposed for their broad-spectrum antiviral activity. The cytidine analog azvudine (FNC) has recently gained attention as a potential treatment for human immunodeficiency virus type 1 (HIV-1) and severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) infection. Considering the distinct substrate specificities of HIV-1 reverse transcriptase (RT) and SARS-CoV-2 RNA-dependent RNA polymerase (RdRp), a unifying mechanism of inhibition remains elusive. Here, we assessed the inhibitory effects of FNC’s active triphosphate form, FNC-TP, across several viral polymerases. The relative efficiency of FNC-TP incorporation followed the order: HIV-1 RT > hepatitis C virus (HCV) RdRp > respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) RdRp > dengue virus type 2 (DENV-2) RdRp ≫ SARS-CoV-2 RdRp. Its incorporation caused chain-termination in all polymerases tested. Antiviral activity against HIV-1 has previously been demonstrated and is here shown with DENV-2. Collectively, the data show that inhibition of viral polymerases by FNC-TP can translate to antiviral activity against both retroviruses and RNA viruses, but the link is not evident for SARS-CoV-2. FNC-TP is a poor substrate for SARS-CoV-2 RdRp, and FNC lacks significant antiviral activity against SARS-CoV-2 in cell culture.

IMPLEMENTASI KEBIJAKAN PENANGGULANGAN DEMAM BERDARAH DENGUE (DBD) DI DESA KARANGNANAS KECAMATAN SOKARAJA
作者： Muhammad Faiz Ananta Zain, Chamid Sutikno, Ariesta Amanda, Indah Ayu Permana Pribadi, Andi Zaelani
摘要中文翻译在印度尼西亚实施登革热预防措施 (DBD)。Desa Karangnanas、Kecamatan Sokaraja、Kabupaten Banyumas tercatat sebagai desa dengan jumlah kasus DBD tertinggi di Kecamatan Sokaraja pada tahun 2024。Penelitian ini bertujuan untuk mengetahui Implementasi kebijakan penanggulangan DBD di Desa Karangnanas。Penelitian menggunakan 方法 kualitatif dengan pendekatan 桌面riptif。Pengumpulan 数据可观察、记录和记录。信息员进行了有目的的采样、采样、测试和技术分析。Hasil penelitian menunjukkan bahwa Implementasi kebijakan penanggulangan DBD di Desa Karangnanas secara umum telah berjalan cukup baik ditinjau dari aspek komunikasi,夏季日,配置,和结构birokrasi menurut teori George C. IMPLEMENTASI KEBIJAKAN PENANGGULANGAN DEMAM BERDARAH DENGUE (DBD) DI DESA KARANGNANAS KECAMATAN SOKARAJA

Authors: Muhammad Faiz Ananta Zain, Chamid Sutikno, Ariesta Amanda, Indah Ayu Permana Pribadi, Andi Zaelani
Original Abstract
Implementasi kebijakan penanggulangan Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) merupakan upaya penting dalam menekan tingginya angka kasus DBD di Indonesia. Desa Karangnanas, Kecamatan Sokaraja, Kabupaten Banyumas tercatat sebagai desa dengan jumlah kasus DBD tertinggi di Kecamatan Sokaraja pada tahun 2024. Penelitian ini bertujuan untuk mengetahui implementasi kebijakan penanggulangan DBD di Desa Karangnanas. Penelitian menggunakan metode kualitatif dengan pendekatan deskriptif. Pengumpulan data dilakukan melalui observasi, wawancara, dan dokumentasi. Informan penelitian terdiri atas pemerintah desa, petugas puskesmas, kader kesehatan, dan masyarakat yang dipilih menggunakan teknik purposive sampling. Hasil penelitian menunjukkan bahwa implementasi kebijakan penanggulangan DBD di Desa Karangnanas secara umum telah berjalan cukup baik ditinjau dari aspek komunikasi, sumber daya, disposisi, dan struktur birokrasi menurut teori George C. Komunikasi dilakukan melalui penyuluhan kesehatan, kegiatan Pemberantasan Sarang Nyamuk (PSN), serta koordinasi antar pelaksana. Sumber daya didukung oleh tenaga kesehatan, kader, fasilitas, dan anggaran, meskipun masih terdapat keterbatasan sarana dan partisipasi masyarakat. Disposisi pelaksana dilaksanakan melalui komitmen dan tanggung jawab dalam pelaksanaan program, sedangkan struktur birokrasi didukung oleh prosedur operasional standar dan pembagian tugas yang jelas. Faktor pendukung implementasi meliputi koordinasi lintas sektor, dukungan pemerintah desa, keterlibatan kader kesehatan, dan media edukasi masyarakat. Faktor penghambat tersebut meliputi rendahnya partisipasi sebagian masyarakat, luasnya wilayah pengawasan, keterbatasan tenaga pelaksana, serta tingginya beban kerja kader kesehatan. Kata kunci: Implementasi Kebijakan, Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD), Pemberantasan Sarang Nyamuk (PSN), Partisipasi Masyarakat
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background

气候变化和登革热动态:印度尼西亚泗水的路径分析

Climate Variability and Dengue Dynamics: A Path Analysis in Surabaya, Indonesia
作者： Khuliyah Candraning Diyanah, Ririh Yudhastuti, Lilis Sulistyorini, Heny Arwati, Hari Basuki Notobroto, Rahmat Dapari, Fariani Syahrul, Muhammad Rasyid Ridha, Babucarr Jassey, Aditya Sukma Pawitra
摘要中文翻译简介:登革热是一种对气候敏感的媒介传播疾病。虽然许多研究已经记录了气候变量与登革热发病率之间的关联,但大多数研究都依赖于传统的回归方法,这些方法无法区分直接和间接的气候路径,限制了对环境健康的机制理解和效用。本研究旨在阐明气候变化对泗水登革热发病率的相互影响。这项研究整合了季节时间序列分解和路径分析,以区分直接和间接的气候路径、日照时长和风速。方法:采用回顾性生态设计,将2020年至2024年每月登革热发病数据与降雨量、温度、湿度、日照时长和风速等气象变量相结合。应用季节时间序列分解来表征时间动态,然后在结构方程模型框架内进行路径分析,以量化直接和间接的气候影响。结果和讨论:登革热发病具有明显的季节性模式,在雨季达到高峰。最高温度、相对湿度、日照时间和最小风速对登革热发病率产生显着的直接影响(p < 0.05)。降雨量没有显示出直接关联,但却是影响其他气候变量的关键上游驱动因素。该结构模型解释了登革热发病率差异的 52.1%,强调了相互关联的气候过程的贡献。结论:这些发现表明,热带大都市环境中的登革热动态受复杂的气候介导途径而非孤立的气候预测因子的控制。这项研究提供了新的环境流行病学证据,以支持气候变化和城市环境变化下的气候信息登革热早期预警系统和适应性病媒控制策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是一种对气候敏感的媒介传播疾病,虽然与气候变量的关联已被记录,但传统的回归方法无法区分直接和间接的气候路径,限制了对环境健康的机制理解和效用。本研究旨在阐明气候变化对泗水登革热发病率的相互影响。
设计与对象
采用回顾性生态设计,将 2020 年至 2024 年每月登革热发病率数据与印度尼西亚泗水的降雨量、温度、湿度、日照时长和风速等气象变量相结合。
核心方法
应用季节时间序列分解来表征时间动态,然后在结构方程模型框架内进行路径分析,以量化气候对登革热发病率的直接和间接影响。
主要结果
最高温度、相对湿度、日照持续时间和最小风速对登革热发病率产生显着的直接影响(p < 0.05),而降雨量没有显示出直接关联,而是影响其他气候变量的关键上游驱动因素。结构模型解释了登革热发病率差异的 52.1%。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,热带大都市环境中的登革热动态是由复杂的气候介导途径控制的,而不是孤立的气候预测因素。这项研究提供了新的环境流行病学证据,以支持气候知情的登革热早期预警系统和适应性病媒控制策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要整合多种气候变量的综合监测系统,以更准确地预测城市热带环境中的登革热疫情。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到其生态设计的限制,无法进行因果推断,并且可能会受到无法测量的混杂因素的影响。由于依赖回顾性数据和单一城市环境,证据强度中等。

Climate Variability and Dengue Dynamics: A Path Analysis in Surabaya, Indonesia

Authors: Khuliyah Candraning Diyanah, Ririh Yudhastuti, Lilis Sulistyorini, Heny Arwati, Hari Basuki Notobroto, Rahmat Dapari, Fariani Syahrul, Muhammad Rasyid Ridha, Babucarr Jassey, Aditya Sukma Pawitra
Original Abstract
Introduction: Dengue fever is a climate-sensitive vector-borne disease. While many studies have documented associations between climate variables and dengue incidence, most rely on conventional regression approaches that fail to separate direct and indirect climatic pathways, limiting mechanistic understanding and utility for environmental health. This study aims to elucidate the interconnected effects of climatic variability on dengue incidence in Surabaya. This study integrates seasonal time-series decomposition and path analysis to distinguish direct and indirect climatic pathways, sunlight duration and wind speed. Methods: A retrospective ecological design, monthly dengue incidence data from 2020 to 2024 were integrated with meteorological variables, including rainfall, temperature, humidity, sunlight duration, and wind speed. Seasonal time-series decomposition was applied to characterize temporal dynamics, followed by path analysis within a structural equation modeling framework to quantify direct and indirect climatic effects. Results and Discussion: A pronounced seasonal pattern in dengue incidence, with peaks during the rainy season. Maximum temperature, relative humidity, sunlight duration, and minimum wind speed exerted significant direct effects on dengue incidence (p < 0.05). Rainfall showed no direct association but acted as a key upstream driver influencing other climatic variables. The structural model explained 52.1% of the variance in dengue incidence, highlighting the contribution of interconnected climatic processes. Conclusion: These findings demonstrate that dengue dynamics in tropical metropolitan environments are governed by complex climate-mediated pathways rather than isolated climatic predictors. This study novel environmental epidemiological evidence to support climate-informed dengue early warning systems and adaptive vector control strategies under climate variability and urban environmental change.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever is a climate-sensitive vector-borne disease, and while associations with climate variables are documented, conventional regression approaches fail to separate direct and indirect climatic pathways, limiting mechanistic understanding and utility for environmental health. This study aims to elucidate the interconnected effects of climatic variability on dengue incidence in Surabaya.
Design and population
A retrospective ecological design was used, integrating monthly dengue incidence data from 2020 to 2024 with meteorological variables including rainfall, temperature, humidity, sunlight duration, and wind speed in Surabaya, Indonesia.
Core methods
Seasonal time-series decomposition was applied to characterize temporal dynamics, followed by path analysis within a structural equation modeling framework to quantify direct and indirect climatic effects on dengue incidence.
Main results
Maximum temperature, relative humidity, sunlight duration, and minimum wind speed exerted significant direct effects on dengue incidence (p < 0.05), while rainfall showed no direct association but acted as a key upstream driver influencing other climatic variables. The structural model explained 52.1% of the variance in dengue incidence.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that dengue dynamics in tropical metropolitan environments are governed by complex climate-mediated pathways rather than isolated climatic predictors. This study provides novel environmental epidemiological evidence to support climate-informed dengue early warning systems and adaptive vector control strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the need for integrated surveillance systems that incorporate multiple climate variables to predict dengue outbreaks more accurately in urban tropical settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its ecological design, which precludes causal inference and may be subject to unmeasured confounding. The evidence strength is moderate due to reliance on retrospective data and a single-city setting.

对巴西亚马逊发热患者虫媒病毒的综合监测揭示了复杂的共循环动态和隐藏的病毒多样性

Integrated surveillance of arboviruses in febrile patients from the Brazilian Amazon reveals complex co-circulation dynamics and hidden viral diversity
作者： Karolina Jeaneth Solórzano Chavarría, E. Gomes, B. Chaves, Vanderson Sampaio, A. Silva-Neto, Daniela Brito, Luís Felipe Alho da Silva, Maianne Yasmin Oliveira Dias, L. Sacchetto, Victória Bernardi
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:虫媒病毒感染仍然是巴西亚马逊地区的一个重大公共卫生挑战。重叠的症状、有限的实验室访问以及多种虫媒病毒的传播阻碍了临床诊断。本研究旨在描述巴西马瑙斯发热患者虫媒病毒感染的流行病学、临床、实验室和基因组特征,并利用宏基因组测序探索其他病毒因子。方法:2021 年 2 月至 2023 年 2 月在巴西马瑙斯的一个三级参考中心进行了一项横断面研究。年龄≥5岁、出现皮疹、发烧或发烧史持续<7天且疟疾厚血涂片呈阴性的患者被纳入研究。使用 ELISA 和 RT-qPCR 检测血清样本中的登革热病毒 (DENV)、寨卡病毒 (ZIKV)、基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV)、黄热病病毒 (YF)、奥罗普切病毒 (OROV) 和马亚罗病毒 (MAYV)。对阳性样本进行基于扩增子的基因组测序以进行系统发育分析。使用从头鸟枪宏基因组测序分析 RT-qPCR 阴性样本的子集。结果:在 708 名入组参与者中,243 名(34.3%)有实验室确诊的虫媒病毒感染:92 名(37.9%)DENV、64 名(26.3%)CHIKV 和 4 名(1.6%)ZIKV,而 83 名(34.2%)实验室资料符合双重感染,主要是 DENV+CHIKV(55/83;66.3%)。确定了 DENV-1 基因型 V 和 DENV-2 基因型 III(亚裔美国人)和 II(国际大都会)的循环。对 35 个样本的宏基因组分析检测到人型佩吉病毒和灵长类红细小病毒 1. 结论:这些发现表明马瑙斯存在复杂的虫媒病毒共循环,并支持结合分子、血清学和基因组方法的综合监测策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于重叠的临床表现、有限的诊断能力以及多种病毒的共同传播,虫媒病毒感染对巴西亚马逊地区造成了持续的公共卫生负担。该研究解决了巴西马瑙斯发热患者虫媒病毒感染特征的需要,该地区的诊断挑战阻碍了准确识别。
设计与对象
2021 年 2 月至 2023 年 2 月在巴西马瑙斯的一个三级参考中心进行了一项横断面研究。年龄为 5 岁或以上、有皮疹、发烧或发烧史持续不到 7 天、疟疾厚血涂片呈阴性的患者被纳入研究。
核心方法
使用 ELISA 和 RT-qPCR 检测血清样本中的登革热病毒 (DENV)、寨卡病毒 (ZIKV)、基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV)、黄热病病毒 (YF)、奥罗普切病毒 (OROV) 和马亚罗病毒 (MAYV)。阳性样本进行了基于扩增子的基因组测序以进行系统发育分析,而 RT-qPCR 阴性样本的子集则使用从头鸟枪法宏基因组测序进行分析。
主要结果
在 708 名参与者中,243 名(34.3%)人患有实验室确诊的虫媒病毒感染:92 名(37.9%)DENV、64 名(26.3%)CHIKV 和 4 名(1.6%)ZIKV。在 83 例 (34.2%) 病例中检测到混合感染,主要是 DENV+CHIKV (55/83;66.3%)。鉴定出 DENV-1 基因型 V 和 DENV-2 基因型 III(亚裔美国人)和 II(国际大都会)。对 35 个样本的宏基因组分析检测到人型佩吉病毒和灵长类红细小病毒 1。
解释与创新
该研究证明了马瑙斯虫媒病毒的复杂共循环,并强调了结合分子、血清学和基因组方法的综合监测的实用性。对人型佩吉病毒和灵长类红细小病毒 1 型的新检测扩大了发热患者中已知的病毒多样性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调需要采取综合诊断策略来改善虫媒病毒流行地区的监测和患者管理。其他病毒因子的检测表明发热性疾病中非虫媒病毒病原体的诊断可能存在不足。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计和单中心设置的限制,这可能会限制普遍性。依赖 RT-qPCR 和 ELISA 进行初步检测会带来潜在的假阴性,而且宏基因组测序仅应用于一小部分样本,限制了更广泛的结论。

Integrated surveillance of arboviruses in febrile patients from the Brazilian Amazon reveals complex co-circulation dynamics and hidden viral diversity

Authors: Karolina Jeaneth Solórzano Chavarría, E. Gomes, B. Chaves, Vanderson Sampaio, A. Silva-Neto, Daniela Brito, Luís Felipe Alho da Silva, Maianne Yasmin Oliveira Dias, L. Sacchetto, Victória Bernardi
Original Abstract
ABSTRACT Background: Arboviral infections continue to be a significant public health challenge in the Brazilian Amazon. Overlapping symptoms, limited laboratory access, and the circulation of multiple arboviruses hamper clinical diagnosis. This study aimed to characterize the epidemiological, clinical, laboratory and genomic profiles of arboviral infections in febrile patients in Manaus, Brazil, and explore additional viral agents using metagenomic sequencing. Methods: A cross-sectional study was conducted between February 2021 and February 2023 at a tertiary reference center in Manaus, Brazil. Patients aged ≥ 5 years of age presenting with a rash and either a fever or a history of fever lasting <7 days and a negative thick blood smear for malaria were enrolled. Serum samples were tested for dengue virus (DENV), Zika virus (ZIKV), Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), yellow fever virus (YF), Oropouche virus (OROV), and Mayaro virus (MAYV) using ELISA and RT-qPCR. Positive samples were subjected to amplicon-based genome sequencing for phylogenetic analysis. A subset of RT-qPCR negative samples was analyzed using de novo shotgun metagenomic sequencing. Results: Among the 708 enrolled participants, 243 (34.3%) had a laboratory-confirmed arboviral infection: 92 (37.9%) DENV, 64 (26.3%) CHIKV, and 4 (1.6%) ZIKV, while 83 (34.2%) laboratory profiles were compatible with coinfection, predominantly DENV+CHIKV (55/83; 66.3%). Circulation of DENV-1 genotype V and DENV-2 genotypes III (Asian American) and II (Cosmopolitan) was identified. Metagenomic analysis of 35 samples detected Pegivirus hominis and Erythroparvovirus primate 1. Conclusions: These findings demonstrate complex arbovirus co-circulation in Manaus and support integrated surveillance strategies combining molecular, serological, and genomic approaches.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Arboviral infections pose a persistent public health burden in the Brazilian Amazon due to overlapping clinical presentations, limited diagnostic capacity, and co-circulation of multiple viruses. The study addressed the need to characterize arboviral infections in febrile patients in Manaus, Brazil, where diagnostic challenges hinder accurate identification.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted from February 2021 to February 2023 at a tertiary reference center in Manaus, Brazil. Patients aged 5 years or older with rash and either fever or a history of fever lasting less than 7 days, and a negative thick blood smear for malaria, were enrolled.
Core methods
Serum samples were tested for dengue virus (DENV), Zika virus (ZIKV), Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), yellow fever virus (YF), Oropouche virus (OROV), and Mayaro virus (MAYV) using ELISA and RT-qPCR. Positive samples underwent amplicon-based genome sequencing for phylogenetic analysis, while a subset of RT-qPCR-negative samples was analyzed using de novo shotgun metagenomic sequencing.
Main results
Among 708 participants, 243 (34.3%) had laboratory-confirmed arboviral infections: 92 (37.9%) DENV, 64 (26.3%) CHIKV, and 4 (1.6%) ZIKV. Coinfections were detected in 83 (34.2%) cases, predominantly DENV+CHIKV (55/83; 66.3%). DENV-1 genotype V and DENV-2 genotypes III (Asian American) and II (Cosmopolitan) were identified. Metagenomic analysis of 35 samples detected Pegivirus hominis and Erythroparvovirus primate 1.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates complex co-circulation of arboviruses in Manaus and highlights the utility of integrated surveillance combining molecular, serological, and genomic approaches. Novel detection of Pegivirus hominis and Erythroparvovirus primate 1 expands the known viral diversity in febrile patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore the need for integrated diagnostic strategies to improve arbovirus surveillance and patient management in endemic regions. The detection of additional viral agents suggests potential underdiagnosis of non-arboviral pathogens in febrile illness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and single-center setting, which may restrict generalizability. Reliance on RT-qPCR and ELISA for primary detection introduces potential false negatives, and metagenomic sequencing was applied to a small subset of samples, limiting broader conclusions.

Enfermedades transmitidas por vectores: chagas, dengue, malaria y hantavirus

Original Abstract
Rol estratégico de la Atención Primaria: el personal médico de familia es fundamental para el cribado inicial, el manejo ambulatorio de los casos leves y la prevención del colapso hospitalario. 农村登革热动态:哥斯达黎加气候、建筑环境和农业的相互作用

Rural dengue dynamics: the interplay of climate, built environment, and agriculture in Costa Rica
作者： Glidden, C. K., Southworth, E. K., Shragai, T., Rojas-Araya, D., Troyo, A., Chaves-Gonzalez, L. E., Marin, R., Vargas Roldan, I., Mordecai, E. A.
摘要中文翻译登革热是世界上负担最重的虫媒病毒疾病之一。尽管通常被认为是一种城市疾病,但许多地区的农村地区都面临着巨大的登革热负担。长期气候、短期天气变化、当地建筑环境和土地利用梯度对农村地区登革热动态的综合影响仍知之甚少,限制了我们预测全球变化下风险转移的能力。在这里,我们调查了哥斯达黎加的这些动态,以阐明这些相互作用的社会环境因素如何影响农村登革热传播。我们首先使用 22 年的州级 (admin-2) 病例数据来确定农村地区的登革热病例和发病率始终高于城市地区。然后,利用十年来的区级 (admin-3) 每月病例数据和贝叶斯分层建模框架,我们确定了与登革热风险最密切相关的气候和土地利用特征。温度是登革热城乡分布的广泛空间模式的基础,而沿海之间的降水影响不同,反映了沿海气候区的对比,而不是城市化和水资源可用性之间的相互作用。在适宜的气候条件下,即使是适度的基础设施建设也会显着增加风险,但这种关系在建筑量较高的情况下会趋于稳定。在低海拔和中海拔农作物覆盖率较高的地区,登革热风险也较高,但在较高、较冷的海拔地区则不然。这些结果共同表明,哥斯达黎加农村登革热的高风险是由于气候适宜性与已建基础设施的基线水平一致造成的,而农业可能成为农村登革热传播的独特驱动因素。

设计与对象
我们首先使用 22 年的州级 (admin-2) 病例数据来确定农村地区的登革热病例和发病率始终高于城市地区。

然后,利用十年来的区级 (admin-3) 每月病例数据和贝叶斯分层建模框架,我们确定了与登革热风险最密切相关的气候和土地利用特征。

科研与公卫意义
在低海拔和中海拔农作物覆盖率较高的地区,登革热风险也较高,但在较高、较冷的海拔地区则不然。
局限与证据强度

Rural dengue dynamics: the interplay of climate, built environment, and agriculture in Costa Rica

Authors: Glidden, C. K., Southworth, E. K., Shragai, T., Rojas-Araya, D., Troyo, A., Chaves-Gonzalez, L. E., Marin, R., Vargas Roldan, I., Mordecai, E. A.
Original Abstract
Dengue is one of the worlds highest-burden arboviral diseases. Although classically considered an urban disease, many regions experience a substantial dengue burden in rural areas. The combined influence of long-term climate, short-term weather variation, local built environments, and land-use gradients on dengue dynamics in rural settings remains poorly understood, limiting our ability to predict shifting risk under global change. Here, we investigate these dynamics in Costa Rica to disentangle how these interacting socio-environmental factors shape rural dengue transmission. We first use 22 years of canton-level (admin-2) case data to establish that both dengue cases and incidence are consistently higher in rural than in urban districts. Then, using ten years of district-level (admin-3) monthly case data and a Bayesian hierarchical modeling framework, we identify the climatic and land-use features most strongly associated with dengue risk. Temperature underlies broad spatial patterns in dengues urban-rural distribution, while precipitation effects differ between coasts, reflecting intercoastal climate zone contrasts rather than interactions between urbanization and water availability. Given suitable climate, even modest levels of built infrastructure substantially increase risk, but the relationship plateaus at higher levels of building volume. Dengue risk is also elevated in areas with high agricultural crop cover at low and mid elevations but not at higher, cooler elevations. Together these results suggest that high risk of rural dengue in Costa Rica result from climate suitability aligning with baseline levels of built infrastructure, with agriculture potentially emerging as a distinct driver of rural dengue transmission.

使用不同的鸟枪宏基因组数据分析流程探索伊蚊的病毒多样性。

Exploration of viral diversity in Aedes mosquitoes employing different shotgun metagenomic data analysis pipelines.
作者： Ekta Gupta, Shashi Sharma, Sachin Narayan Tikar, Paban Kumar Dash
摘要中文翻译宏基因组学或元病毒组学正在成为病原体监测和大流行防范的强大技术。蚊子是传播许多新出现的病毒的重要载体,这些病毒导致了许多疫情的爆发。监测蚊子对于研究其病毒组至关重要,这有助于了解疾病动态并采取预防措施。在本研究中,使用 Ion GeneStudio S5 系统的鸟枪法宏基因组方法经过优化,可探索病毒多样性。2024 年季风后季节,从印度中部收集了总共 1913 只伊蚊幼虫。将伊蚊幼虫饲养至成年,并使用 Ion Torrent S5 平台进行测序。使用三个生物信息学管道进行计算分析:Chan Zuckerberg ID (CZ ID)、Genome Detective Platform 和 Galaxy Platform。创建了 11 种已知病毒的模拟数据库,并使用公开可用的 NCBI 序列读取存档 (SRA) 数据集来验证所有三个管道。在检测准确性方面,Genome Detective 和 CZ ID 表现非常出色,因此可能适合未来的蚊子病毒组监测研究。我们发现病毒的存在。Alphamesonivirus cavallynense 和 Phasivirus phasiense 在所有样本中占主导地位。CZ ID在1个样本中检出登革热病毒,而样本中最常见的是温州索贝莫样病毒、湖北蚊子病毒2型、细胞融合剂病毒。发现的其他病毒如伊蚊病毒(AeAV;糖病毒属)、伊蚊托蒂病毒、Verdadero 病毒和 Chaq 样病毒。不同管道之间结果的差异可能归因于不同病毒参考数据库、分类算法、指标和分析参数的结合。据我们所知,这项研究是印度首次使用 Ion GeneStudio S5 平台对蚊子进行宏基因组研究。研究结果提供了宏基因组管道的比较评估,并阐明了每个管道的详细信息及其对未来研究的作用。

问题与背景
蚊子是传播许多新出现的病毒的重要载体,这些病毒导致了许多疫情的爆发。
设计与对象
在本研究中,使用 Ion GeneStudio S5 系统的鸟枪法宏基因组方法经过优化,可探索病毒多样性。

伊蚊幼虫饲养至成年并使用 Ion Torrent S5 平台进行测序。
主要结果
CZ ID在1个样本中检出登革热病毒,而样本中最常见的是温州索贝莫样病毒、湖北蚊子病毒2型、细胞融合剂病毒。
解释与创新
据我们所知,这项研究是印度首次使用 Ion GeneStudio S5 平台对蚊子进行宏基因组研究。
科研与公卫意义
在检测准确性方面,Genome Detective 和 CZ ID 表现非常出色,因此可能适合未来的蚊子病毒组监测研究。
局限与证据强度
研究结果提供了宏基因组管道的比较评估,并阐明了每个管道的详细信息及其对未来研究的作用。

Exploration of viral diversity in Aedes mosquitoes employing different shotgun metagenomic data analysis pipelines.

Authors: Ekta Gupta, Shashi Sharma, Sachin Narayan Tikar, Paban Kumar Dash
Original Abstract
Metagenomics or metaviromics is emerging as a powerful technology for pathogen surveillance and pandemic preparedness. Mosquitoes are important vectors for transmission of many emerging viruses responsible for numerous outbreaks. Monitoring mosquitoes becomes essential to investigate its virome which leads to understanding of disease dynamics and allow preventive actions. In this study, shotgun metagenomic methodology using Ion GeneStudio S5 System was optimized for exploration of viral diversity. A total of 1913 Aedes larvae were collected from Central India during post monsoon season of 2024. Aedes larvae reared to adulthood and processed for sequencing using Ion Torrent S5 platform. Computational analyses were performed using three bioinformatic pipelines: Chan Zuckerberg ID (CZ ID), Genome Detective Platform and the Galaxy Platform. A mock database of 11 known viruses was created as well as publicly available NCBI Sequence Read Archive (SRA) datasets were used to validate all three pipelines. In terms of detection accuracy, Genome Detective and CZ ID performed exceptionally well and therefore may be suitable for future mosquito virome surveillance studies. We found presence of viruses viz. Alphamesonivirus cavallynense and Phasivirus phasiense dominating in all samples. Dengue virus was detected in one sample by CZ ID, whereas Wenzhou sobemo-like virus, Hubei mosquito virus 2, Cell fusing agent virus found most commonly among samples. Other viruses found like Aedes anphevirus (AeAV; genus Glybovirus), Aedes totivirus, Verdadero virus and Chaq-like virus. Variation in the result among different pipelines are likely attributable to incorporation of different viral reference databases, classification algorithms, metrics and analysis parameters. To the best of our knowledge, this study represents the first metagenomic study of mosquitoes using Ion GeneStudio S5 platform in India. The findings provide a comparative evaluation of the metagenomic pipelines and elucidates detailed information of each pipeline and its working for future studies.

Scientific and public-health significance

使用经杀虫剂处理的屋檐和窗户屏蔽作为病媒控制工具进行房屋改造:来自坦桑尼亚半田系统的证据和模拟流行病学影响。

House modifications using insecticide-treated screening of eave and window as a vector control tool: evidence from a semi-field system in Tanzania and simulated epidemiological impact.
作者： Olukayode G Odufuwa, Richard J Sheppard, Safina Ngonyani, Ahmadi Bakari Mpelepele, Dickson Kobe, Agathus Njohole, Jason Moore, Jastin Lusoli Lusoli, Joseph B Muganga, Rune Bosselmann
摘要中文翻译背景:未改进的房屋结构中的缝隙,尤其是屋檐和窗户,容易让蚊子进入,增加室内媒介传播疾病的传播。对此类房屋进行简单改造可以减少人类接触,杀虫剂处理可以杀死蚊子,使所有社区成员受益。本研究评估了经杀虫剂处理的屋檐和窗户纱网 (ITS),并与坦桑尼亚经氯菊酯和 PBO 处理的蚊帐进行了比较,并与溴氰菊酯和胡椒基丁醚 (PBO) 进行了比较。方法:在大型网笼内的四个实验小屋​​中采用随机拉丁方设计(4 × 4),以便在小屋内外重新捕获蚊子。评估了四种处理方法:(1)新的(保存 12 个月的)ITS;(2) 12个月自然老化的ITS;(3) 12 个月现场使用的拟除虫菊酯-PBO 蚊帐(坦桑尼亚的标准护理),以及 (4) 不进行治疗。该研究进行了 32 个晚上,每间小屋每晚使用 30 只蚊子。使用四种实验室饲养的菌株:疟疾媒介(阿拉伯按蚊和按蚊)、登革热媒介(埃及伊蚊)和令人讨厌的叮咬媒介(致倦库蚊)。对重新捕获的蚊子进行 72 小时死亡率、吸血情况和进入小屋的评估。使用修改后的机制模型进行模拟,跟踪恶性疟原虫疟疾,以说明这些产品的潜在流行病学影响。结果:与 12 个月老化的拟除虫菊酯-PBO 处理的蚊帐相比,针对所有蚊种,新 ITS 导致更高的死亡率 [比值比:2.25(95% 置信区间:1.65-3.06),p < 0.0001],而老化 ITS 相似 [OR:0.80(95% CI:0.59-1.08),p = 0.141]。新旧 ITS 均显着 (p < 0.0001) 减少蚊子吸血 [新 OR:0.02(95% CI:0.01-0.03);旧OR:0.09(95%CI:0.05-0.14)]和小屋入口[新IRR:0.10(95%CI:0.08-0.13);老化IRR:0.25(95%CI:0.21-0.31)]。传播模型估计表明,ITS 的流行病学影响可能会在人口层面取代经拟除虫菊酯-PBO 处理的蚊帐。模型结果表明影响效力取决于假设的干预覆盖率、持久性和蚊子生物学特性。结论:本研究引入了标准化半场生物测定来评估 ITS,生成昆虫学数据,首次能够对其对疟疾传播的潜在影响进行建模。ITS 是公共卫生的有效工具,因为它可以杀死大量疟疾和登革热媒介,并减少令人讨厌的叮咬。鉴于其简单性,应将其视为筛选未改良房屋的附加或独立工具。

设计与对象

主要结果
对于所有蚊种,与 12 个月老化的拟除虫菊酯-PBO 处理的蚊帐相比,新 ITS 导致更高的死亡率 [比值比:2.25(95% 置信区间:1.65-3.06),p < 0.0001],而老化 ITS 相似 [OR:0.80(95% CI:0.59-1.08),p = 0.141]。

House modifications using insecticide-treated screening of eave and window as a vector control tool: evidence from a semi-field system in Tanzania and simulated epidemiological impact.

Authors: Olukayode G Odufuwa, Richard J Sheppard, Safina Ngonyani, Ahmadi Bakari Mpelepele, Dickson Kobe, Agathus Njohole, Jason Moore, Jastin Lusoli Lusoli, Joseph B Muganga, Rune Bosselmann
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Gaps in unimproved house structures, especially in eaves and windows, allow mosquito entry, increasing indoor vector-borne disease transmission. Simple modifications to such houses may reduce human exposure, and insecticide treatment may kill mosquitoes, benefiting all community members. This study evaluated insecticide-treated screening (ITS) for eaves and windows, incorporated with deltamethrin and piperonyl butoxide (PBO), compared to a permethrin and PBO-treated bednet in Tanzania. METHOD: A randomised Latin-square design (4 × 4) was used in four experimental huts within a large netting cage to allow mosquito recapture inside and outside of huts. Four treatments were evaluated: (1) new (12-month stored) ITS; (2) 12-month naturally-aged ITS; (3) 12-month field-used pyrethroid-PBO bednet (standard of care in Tanzania), and (4) no treatment. The study was performed for 32 nights using 30 mosquitoes per strain, per hut per night. Four laboratory-reared strains were used: malaria vectors (Anopheles arabiensis and An. funestus), dengue vector (Aedes aegypti), and nuisance biting (Culex quinquefasciatus). Recaptured mosquitoes were assessed for mortality at 72 h, blood-feeding, and hut entry. A simulation with a modified mechanistic model tracking Plasmodium falciparum malaria was used to illustrate potential epidemiological impact from these products. RESULTS: Against all mosquito species compared to 12-month aged pyrethroid-PBO-treated bednet, new ITS induced higher mortality [Odds Ratio:2.25(95%Confidence Interval:1.65-3.06),p < 0.0001], and aged-ITS was similar [OR:0.80(95%CI:0.59-1.08),p = 0.141]. Both new and aged ITS significantly (p < 0.0001) reduced mosquito blood-feeding [new OR:0.02(95% CI:0.01-0.03); aged OR:0.09(95%CI:0.05-0.14)] and hut entry [new IRR:0.10(95%CI:0.08-0.13); aged IRR:0.25(95%CI:0.21-0.31)]. Transmission model estimates indicate epidemiological impacts of ITS may supersede pyrethroid-PBO-treated bednets at the population level. The model results indicate impact potency depends on assumed intervention percentage cover, durability, and mosquito bionomics. CONCLUSIONS: This study introduces a standardised semi-field bioassay to evaluate ITS, generating entomological data that, for the first time, enabled modelling of its potential impact on malaria transmission. ITS is an efficacious tool for public health as it kills substantial proportions of malaria and dengue vectors, and reduces nuisance biting. Given its simplicity, it should be considered an additional or stand-alone tool for screening unimproved houses.

栖息地类型和季节变化是城市水生微生境中蚊子增殖的驱动因素

Habitat Type and Seasonal Variation as Drivers of Mosquito Proliferation in Urban Aquatic Microhabitats
作者： S Garrison Carruth, Jenna C Michael, Chalmers Vasquez, John-Paul Mutebi, Maria Litvinova, André Barretto Bruno Wilke
摘要中文翻译摘要 埃及伊蚊是登革热、寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅病毒的主要媒介物种,适合在城市环境中繁衍生息。由于蚊子与人群的距离很近,并且与虫媒病毒感染相关的不良健康后果,因此控制蚊子对于降低疫情和疾病传播的风险至关重要。减少源头和杀幼虫剂的应用是蚊子控制的核心组成部分,因为它们通过针对未成熟的生命阶段来减少成蚊的出现。因此,我们的目标是确定伊蚊使用的城市水生栖息地。埃及伊蚊并评估其对未成熟蚊子生产的贡献的时间变化。为了区分栖息地利用和栖息地生产力,我们将幼虫的存在视为产卵或早期生存的指标,将蛹的存在视为栖息地在大部分未成熟生命周期中支持发育的证据。2018 年 7 月至 2019 年 10 月期间,佛罗里达州迈阿密戴德县进行了 2,482 次检查,发现了 2,756 个水生栖息地,其中 19,466 种白斑蚊。收集了埃及伊蚊幼虫和 3,648 只蛹。在县和栖息地分辨率下,观察到幼虫和蛹的存在和丰度的独特时间趋势。县级趋势显示,夏季幼虫和蛹产量最高,冬季蛹产量最低。然而,不同栖息地的时间模式不同,支持对栖息地有利性的上下文相关解释,其中相同的栖息地类型可能根据当地环境条件支持不同水平的未成熟蚊子生产。我们的结果表明,幼虫频繁出现的栖息地并不总是有大量的蛹存在,这表明幼虫的存在并不一定反映栖息地的生产力。按季节优先考虑与蛹生产相关的栖息地的控制策略可能会改善全年的蚊子控制、疫情爆发准备和资源分配。

提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。

科研与公卫意义
按季节优先考虑与蛹生产相关的栖息地的控制策略可能会改善全年的蚊子控制、疫情爆发准备和资源分配。

Habitat Type and Seasonal Variation as Drivers of Mosquito Proliferation in Urban Aquatic Microhabitats

Authors: S Garrison Carruth, Jenna C Michael, Chalmers Vasquez, John-Paul Mutebi, Maria Litvinova, André Barretto Bruno Wilke
Original Abstract
Abstract Aedes aegypti is the primary vector species for dengue, Zika, and chikungunya viruses, and is adapted to thrive in urban environments. Their proximity to human populations and the adverse health outcomes associated with arboviral infections make mosquito control essential for reducing the risk of outbreaks and disease spread. Source reduction and larvicide applications are central components of mosquito control because they reduce adult mosquito emergence by targeting immature life stages. Therefore, our objective was to identify urban aquatic habitats used by Ae. aegypti and assess temporal variation in their contribution to immature mosquito production. To distinguish habitat use from habitat productivity, we considered larval presence as an indicator of oviposition or early-stage survival and pupal presence as evidence that a habitat supported development through most of the immature life cycle. Between July 2018 and October 2019, 2,482 inspections in Miami-Dade County, Florida, identified 2,756 aquatic habitats from which 19,466 Ae. aegypti larvae and 3,648 pupae were collected. Unique temporal trends were observed for both larval and pupal presence and abundance at county and habitat resolutions. County-level trends showed the highest larval and pupal production during Summer and the lowest pupal production during Winter. However, temporal patterns differed across habitats, supporting a context-dependent interpretation of habitat conduciveness, in which the same habitat type may support different levels of immature mosquito production depending on local environmental conditions. Our results show that habitats with frequent larval occurrence did not consistently have high pupal presence, indicating that larval presence does not necessarily reflect habitat productivity. Control strategies that prioritize habitats repeatedly associated with pupal production by season may improve year-round mosquito control, outbreak preparedness, and resource allocation.

社论:综合基因组和系统方法揭示病毒传染病中宿主病毒的相互作用

Editorial: Integrated genomic and systems approaches to unravel host-virus interactions in viral infectious diseases
作者： Samuel Pushparaj, Megan Justice, Raja Angamuthu, Esteban A. Hernandez-Vargas
摘要中文翻译两项贡献研究了针对中枢神经系统 (CNS) 的病毒。对 69 名水痘带状疱疹病毒 (VZV) 中枢神经系统感染患者的脑脊液 (CSF) 进行的临床和蛋白质组学分析记录了头痛、发烧和运动/感觉障碍的主要表现。CSF 蛋白质组学显示,相对于对照,CSF 蛋白质丰度显着升高,上调的蛋白质富含 IFN-1 信号传导、细胞焦亡和血脑屏障 (BBB) 破坏途径。严重脑炎病例中的补体系统过度激活成为疾病严重程度的候选标志物和潜在的治疗靶点,强调了脑脊液蛋白质组学在识别可操作分子特征方面的价值。寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 的多系统转录组学研究整合了来自受感染患者的人脑类器官、单核细胞衍生的树突状细胞和 PBMC 的数据,以绘制跨感染阶段的干扰素刺激基因 (ISG) 调控图谱。IFN-β 治疗有效激活了脑类器官中的 ISG,包括 IRF7 和 IFITM3,而单细胞转录组学揭示了 ISG 反应的细胞异质性。跨系统分析确定了 22 个共享 ISG,LASSO 回归提名了 11 个候选诊断生物标志物。功能性 siRNA 敲低证实 IFI35、IFI44 和 OAS3 限制 ZIKV 复制,将转录组学发现与经过验证的抗病毒机制联系起来。埃博拉病毒病 (EVD) 的机器学习和数学模型研究解决了关于 IFN-I 在疾病结果中的作用的长期争议。对 2014-2016 年西非流行病患者数据的分析表明,幸存者中的 IFN-I 反应先于症状出现,而非幸存者则在 3-4 天后出现相同的反应。这种延迟导致 IFN-I 和 IL-12 信号在非幸存者中重叠,从而破坏了有效 T 细胞激活所需的连续细胞因子线索。这些发现将时间免疫动态提升为宿主保护与病理的一个关键但未被充分认识的决定因素。登革热病毒 (DENV) 感染的综合代谢组学-转录组学研究使用 LC-MS 和 GC-MS 对 41 名患者和 23 名健康对照者的血浆进行了分析,鉴定出 197 种差异丰富的代谢物。通过 ROC 分析,建议将上调最多的物种(包括反式肉桂酸和左旋乙酰肉碱)作为诊断生物标志物。KEGG 和 GSEA 分析强调 ATP 结合盒 (ABC) 转运蛋白的破坏是一个关键的代谢扰动,GEO 数据集的转录组整合将 ABCC5、ABCB1 和 ABCG5 识别为中心目标,证实了这一点,为理解 DENV 诱导的细胞病理学开辟了新途径。一项针对 157 名 COVID-19 患者的纵向研究检查了不同疾病严重程度组中 TLR7、TYK2 和 OAS1 的表达。TLR7 低表达与严重程度和病毒血症密切相关,而 OAS1 在病毒载量较高的重症病例中持续存在,即使 TLR7 在恢复过程中下降。这些先天免疫基因的相互依赖性凸显了针对 SARS-CoV-2 的早期抗病毒反应的协调性和严重性分层性质,并对预后分层具有影响。一项研究探讨了转录因子 EB (TFEB) 和 E3 (TFE3)(溶酶体生物发生的主要调节因子)如何调节三种冠状病毒属的病毒复制、自噬和细胞凋亡:γ 冠状病毒 IBV、β 冠状病毒 HCoV-OC43 和 α 冠状病毒 PEDV。感染上调 TFEB/TFE3 并诱导溶酶体应激反应。TFE3 敲低揭示了其在控制复制和细胞死亡中的主要作用,在 IBV 和 HCoV-OC43 感染的细胞中具有协同 TFEB/TFE3 效应。Brefeldin A 实验进一步证实 HCoV-OC43 的流出是通过溶酶体途径发生的,从而将 TFEB/TFE3 控制的溶酶体生物学确定为跨冠状病毒属的保守宿主脆弱性。非洲猪瘟病毒 (ASFV) 的转录组学研究解决了其对原代猪肺泡巨噬细胞 (PAM) 的严格趋向性,而不是在永生化巨噬细胞系 (iPAM) 中的有限复制。SY-1 菌株的比较分析揭示了不同的宿主反应:iPAM 主要参与脂质代谢和氧化应激,而 PAM 则激活细胞因子信号传导和广泛的免疫效应程序。因此,iPAM 中复制的减少反映了根本不同的细胞环境,而不仅仅是受体的可用性。功能验证将 JPH4 和 CYP1A1 确定为候选宿主许可因子,为改进体外模型和抗病毒策略奠定了基础,鉴于缺乏获得许可的 ASF 疫苗,这一点至关重要。这七篇文章共同证明了集成多组学和系统方法解码宿主病毒相互作用的力量。交叉主题包括 IFN-I 信号传导的中心性和背景依赖性、时间免疫动力学的重要性、多组学整合在生物标志物发现中的实用性,以及适当的细胞和患者模型对机制研究的价值。我们感谢所有贡献的作者和审稿人,并希望该合集能够促进病毒传染病的新型诊断和宿主靶向治疗的发展。关键词:宿主-病毒相互作用、基因组学、转录组学、蛋白质组学、代谢组学、干扰素信号传导、先天免疫、病毒传染病、系统生物学、多组学

Editorial: Integrated genomic and systems approaches to unravel host-virus interactions in viral infectious diseases

Authors: Samuel Pushparaj, Megan Justice, Raja Angamuthu, Esteban A. Hernandez-Vargas
Original Abstract
Two contributions examine viruses targeting the central nervous system (CNS). A clinical and proteomic analysis of cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) from 69 patients with varicella-zoster virus (VZV) CNS infection documents the predominant manifestations of headache, fever, and motor/sensory disturbances. CSF proteomics revealed markedly elevated protein abundance relative to controls, with upregulated proteins enriched in IFN-1 signaling, pyroptosis, and blood-brain barrier (BBB) disruption pathways. Complement system overactivation in severe encephalitis cases emerged as a candidate marker of disease severity and a potential therapeutic target, underscoring the value of CSF proteomics for identifying actionable molecular signatures. A multi-system transcriptomic study of Zika virus (ZIKV) integrates data from human brain organoids, monocyte-derived dendritic cells, and PBMCs from infected patients to map interferonstimulated gene (ISG) regulation across infection stages. IFN-β treatment potently activated ISGs including IRF7 and IFITM3 in brain organoids, while single-cell transcriptomics revealed cellular heterogeneity in ISG responses. Cross-system analysis identified 22 shared ISGs, and LASSO regression nominated 11 diagnostic biomarker candidates. Functional siRNA knockdown confirmed that IFI35, IFI44, and OAS3 restrict ZIKV replication, bridging transcriptomic discovery to validated antiviral mechanisms.A machine learning and mathematical modeling study of Ebola virus disease (EVD) resolves a longstanding controversy over the role of IFN-I in disease outcome. Analysis of patient data from the 2014-2016 West Africa epidemic reveals that IFN-I responses precede symptom onset in survivors, whereas non-survivors mount equivalent responses 3-4 days later. This delay causes IFN-I and IL-12 signals to overlap in non-survivors, disrupting the sequential cytokine cues required for effective T cell activation. The findings elevate temporal immune dynamics as a critical yet underappreciated determinant of host protection versus pathology.An integrated metabolomics-transcriptomics study of dengue virus (DENV) infection profiles plasma from 41 patients and 23 healthy controls using LC-MS and GC-MS, identifying 197 differentially abundant metabolites. Top upregulated species including trans-cinnamic acid and Lacetylcarnitine are proposed as diagnostic biomarkers by ROC analysis. KEGG and GSEA analyses highlighted disruption of ATP-binding cassette (ABC) transporters as a key metabolic perturbation, corroborated by transcriptomic integration from GEO datasets identifying ABCC5, ABCB1, and ABCG5 as hub targets, opening a new avenue for understanding DENV-induced cellular pathology.A longitudinal study of 157 COVID-19 patients examines expression of TLR7, TYK2, and OAS1 across disease severity groups. Low TLR7 expression robustly correlates with severity and viremia, while OAS1 persists in severe cases with high viral loads even as TLR7 declines during recovery. The interdependence of these innate immune genes highlights the coordinated yet severity-stratified nature of early antiviral responses to SARS-CoV-2 and has implications for prognostic stratification.A study examines how transcription factors EB (TFEB) and E3 (TFE3), master regulators of lysosomal biogenesis, modulate viral replication, autophagy, and apoptosis across three coronavirus genera: gamma coronavirus IBV, beta coronavirus HCoV-OC43, and alpha coronavirus PEDV. Infection upregulates TFEB/TFE3 and induces a lysosomal stress response. TFE3 knockdown reveals its predominant role in controlling replication and cell death, with synergistic TFEB/TFE3 effects in IBV-and HCoV-OC43-infected cells. Brefeldin A experiments further establish that HCoV-OC43 egress occurs via the lysosomal pathway, identifying TFEB/TFE3-governed lysosomal biology as a conserved host vulnerability across coronavirus genera.A transcriptomic investigation of African swine fever virus (ASFV) addresses its strict tropism for primary porcine alveolar macrophages (PAMs) versus limited replication in immortalized macrophage lines (iPAMs). Comparative profiling of the SY-1 strain reveals divergent host responses: iPAMs predominantly engage lipid metabolism and oxidative stress, whereas PAMs activate cytokine signaling and broad immune effector programs. Reduced replication in iPAMs thus reflects a fundamentally different cellular environment, not merely receptor availability. Functional validation identifies JPH4 and CYP1A1 as candidate host permissiveness factors, providing a foundation for improved in vitro models and antiviral strategies-critical given the absence of licensed ASF vaccines.Together, these seven articles demonstrate the power of integrated multi-omics and systems approaches to decode host-virus interactions. Cross-cutting themes include the centrality and context-dependence of IFN-I signaling, the importance of temporal immune dynamics, the utility of multi-omics integration for biomarker discovery, and the value of appropriate cell and patient models for mechanistic investigation. We thank all contributing authors and reviewers, and hope this collection advances the development of novel diagnostics and host-targeted therapeutics for viral infectious diseases.Keywords: host-virus interactions, genomics, transcriptomics, proteomics, metabolomics, interferon signaling, innate immunity, viral infectious diseases, systems biology, multi-omics

利用宿主 U2AF1 和 U2AF2 剪接因子可促进蚊媒正黄病毒跨物种感染。

Exploitation of host U2AF1 and U2AF2 splicing factors facilitates mosquito-borne orthoflavivirus infection across species.
作者： Changbai Huang, Yiquan Cai, Cancan Chen, Xuanfeng Zhu, Ying’an Liang, Yingrui Luo, Qinyu Peng, Yi Wang, Chao Yang, Ming Li
摘要中文翻译为了确定正黄病毒复制所必需的新宿主因子,我们对从感染登革热病毒或寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 的细胞中分离的内质网组分进行了蛋白质组学分析。在富集的蛋白质中,剪接因子 U2AF2 及其异二聚体伴侣 U2AF1 对有效的病毒感染至关重要,在病毒蛋白质合成阶段发挥作用。U2AF1 的精氨酸/丝氨酸丰富 (RS) 结构域和第一个锌指节 (Zn1),以及除 U2AF2 的 RS 结构域之外的几乎所有结构域,对于其原病毒功能至关重要。U2AF1-U2AF2 相互作用的破坏可有效抑制 ZIKV 感染。U2AF1和U2AF2部分定位于细胞质,值得注意的是,细胞质U2AF2主要是截短的形式,足以支持病毒复制。U2AF1 和 U2AF2 均与病毒 RNA 结合,其中 U2AF1 结合依赖于 U2AF2。有趣的是,将埃及伊蚊同源物u2af38反式互补到U2AF1敲除细胞中可以恢复ZIKV复制,而在U2AF2敲除细胞中表达u2af50则不能。然而,u2af38 或 u2af50 的敲低显着抑制了蚊子中黄病毒的复制。总之,这些发现揭示了黄病毒在人类细胞和蚊子中共同选择宿主剪接因子,强调了病毒利用的保守的跨物种机制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
U2AF1 的精氨酸/丝氨酸丰富 (RS) 结构域和第一个锌指节 (Zn1),以及除 U2AF2 的 RS 结构域之外的几乎所有结构域,对于其原病毒功能至关重要。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
为了确定正黄病毒复制所必需的新宿主因子,我们对从感染登革热病毒或寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 的细胞中分离的内质网组分进行了蛋白质组学分析。
主要结果
在富集的蛋白质中,剪接因子 U2AF2 及其异二聚体伴侣 U2AF1 对有效的病毒感染至关重要,在病毒蛋白质合成阶段发挥作用。
解释与创新
U2AF1和U2AF2部分定位于细胞质,值得注意的是,细胞质U2AF2主要是截短的形式,足以支持病毒复制。
科研与公卫意义
然而,u2af38 或 u2af50 的敲低显着抑制了蚊子中黄病毒的复制。
局限与证据强度
总之,这些发现揭示了黄病毒在人类细胞和蚊子中共同选择宿主剪接因子,强调了病毒利用的保守的跨物种机制。

Exploitation of host U2AF1 and U2AF2 splicing factors facilitates mosquito-borne orthoflavivirus infection across species.

Authors: Changbai Huang, Yiquan Cai, Cancan Chen, Xuanfeng Zhu, Ying’an Liang, Yingrui Luo, Qinyu Peng, Yi Wang, Chao Yang, Ming Li
Original Abstract
To identify novel host factors essential for orthoflavivirus replication, we performed proteomic profiling of endoplasmic reticulum fractions isolated from cells infected with Dengue virus or Zika virus (ZIKV). Among the enriched proteins, the splicing factor U2AF2 and its heterodimeric partner U2AF1 were found to be critical for efficient viral infection, functioning at the viral protein synthesis stage. The arginine/serine-rich (RS) domain and the first zinc knuckle (Zn1) of U2AF1, as well as nearly all domains except the RS domain of U2AF2, were essential for their proviral function. Disruption of the U2AF1-U2AF2 interaction potently suppressed ZIKV infection. U2AF1 and U2AF2 partially localize to the cytoplasm, and notably, the cytoplasmic U2AF2 was predominantly a truncated form that was sufficient to support viral replication. Both U2AF1 and U2AF2 bind to viral RNA, with U2AF1 binding being dependent on U2AF2. Interestingly, trans-complementation of the Aedes aegypti homolog u2af38 into U2AF1-knockout cells restored ZIKV replication, whereas expressing u2af50 in U2AF2-knockdown cells did not. However, knockdown of either u2af38 or u2af50 significantly inhibited the flavivirus replication in mosquitoes. Together, these findings reveal that flaviviruses co-opt host splicing factors in both human cells and mosquitoes, underscoring a conserved cross-species mechanism of viral exploitation.

一种基于社区的传染病棋盘游戏,旨在加强老年护理教育的代际学习。

A Community-Based Infectious Disease Board Game to Enhance Intergenerational Learning in Gerontological Nursing Education.
作者： Chun-Chin Tsai, Mei-Fang Chen
摘要中文翻译目的:开发和评估基于社区的传染病棋盘游戏干预措施,旨在加强护生和老年人之间的代际学习,并提高与登革热、疥疮、SARS 和艾滋病毒/艾滋病相关的能力。设计:这项准实验研究采用独立、非匹配、两组前测-后测设计。方法:招募了台湾南部的三年级护理学生,并将其分配到干预组或对照组。干预组参加了为期五周的计划,其中包括三周的课堂教学,然后是与老年人一起进行的两周的基于棋盘游戏的学习课程。对照组接受传统的讲座式教学。测量的结果包括学生的沟通、团队合作和社会责任,以及老年人对传染病的知识、态度和预防行为。结果:干预组的学生在健康沟通(t = 2.82,p = .006)、团队协作(t = 5.33,p < .001)和社会责任(t = 4.07,p < .001)方面比对照组表现出显着更大的进步。老年人在传染病知识、态度和预防行为方面也表现出显着改善,所有 p < .001。结论:将教育棋盘游戏融入护理课程是一种有效且可扩展的策略,可增强代际学习、培养学生的关键专业能力以及提高老年人的健康素养。临床相关性:该方法通过促进代际合作、加强社区参与和支持老年人预防性健康行为的发展,为老年护理教育提供了实际意义。

A Community-Based Infectious Disease Board Game to Enhance Intergenerational Learning in Gerontological Nursing Education.

Authors: Chun-Chin Tsai, Mei-Fang Chen
Original Abstract
PURPOSE: To develop and evaluate a community-based infectious disease board game intervention aimed at enhancing intergenerational learning between nursing students and older adults, and improving competencies related to dengue fever, scabies, SARS, and HIV/AIDS. DESIGN: This quasi-experimental study used an independent, non-matched, two-group pretest - posttest design. METHODS: Third-year nursing students in southern Taiwan were recruited and assigned to either an intervention or a control group. The intervention group participated in a five-week program consisting of three weeks of classroom instruction followed by two weeks of board game - based learning sessions conducted with older adults. The control group received traditional lecture-based instruction. Outcomes measured included students' communication, teamwork, and social responsibility, as well as older adults' knowledge, attitudes, and preventive behaviors regarding infectious diseases. FINDINGS: Students in the intervention group demonstrated significantly greater improvements than the control group in health communication (t = 2.82, p = .006), team collaboration (t = 5.33, p < .001), and social responsibility (t = 4.07, p < .001). Older adults also showed significant improvements in infectious disease knowledge, attitudes, and preventive behaviors, all p < .001. CONCLUSIONS: The integration of educational board games into nursing curricula is an effective and scalable strategy for enhancing intergenerational learning, fostering key professional competencies among students, and improving health literacy among older adults. CLINICAL RELEVANCE: This approach provides practical implications for gerontological nursing education by promoting intergenerational collaboration, strengthening community engagement, and supporting the development of preventive health behaviors among older populations.

Design and population

大肠杆菌衍生的银纳米颗粒对印度南部伊蚊病媒控制的评价

Evaluation of Escherichia coli-derived Silver Nanoparticles for Aedes Vector Control in Southern India
作者： Rajalakshmi Anbalagan, Mortein Henri, M. Priyadharshini, Farhat Syed Kaseem, Sathya Jeevitha, Balachandar Vellingiri, Anand Setty Balakrishnan, Neena Elezebeth Philip, Mansi Malik, Sulochana Shekhar
摘要中文翻译背景:埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊是泰米尔纳德邦传播登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒和黄热病等主要虫媒病毒疾病的主要蚊媒。埃及伊蚊是主要媒介,其次是白纹伊蚊。由于可预测的化学杀幼虫剂的局限性和环境问题,迫切需要替代和可持续的病媒控制策略。方法:在这项研究中,利用大肠杆菌通过生物方法合成了环保型银纳米颗粒,该方法开发了细菌的自然还原和稳定特性。使用紫外-可见光谱和傅里叶变换红外(FTIR)光谱对合成的银纳米颗粒的尺寸、形状和稳定性进行了表征。针对印度泰米尔纳德邦蒂鲁瓦鲁尔地区的埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊幼虫,评估了银纳米粒子的杀幼虫功效。LC50和LC90值是根据SPSS中的芬尼法(Miller-Tainter法)使用概率分析来估计的。结果:生物合成的银纳米粒子对两种蚊子均表现出很强的杀幼虫活性,根据剂量方法,白纹伊蚊的幼虫死亡率低于埃及伊蚊。埃及伊蚊的半致死浓度 (LC50) 估计为 270 ± 16 mg/L,白纹伊蚊的半致死浓度 (LC50) 估计为 194 ± 13 mg/L,埃及伊蚊的半致死浓度 (LC90) 估计为 3480 ± 490 mg/L,白纹伊蚊的 LC90 值估计为 2510 ± 360 mg/L。研究结果表明,生物合成的银纳米颗粒是化学杀幼虫剂的有效替代品,可提高功效并减少对环境的影响。结论:当前的研究强调了利用生物资源合成纳米颗粒和实施可持续方法控制蚊媒疾病的双重优势。未来的研究应侧重于大规模生产、现场功效评估以及对非目标生物体的潜在影响的评估,以支持实际应用。

问题与背景
埃及伊蚊是主要媒介,其次是白纹伊蚊。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,使用大肠杆菌通过生物方法合成了环保的银纳米粒子,该方法开发了细菌的自然还原和稳定特性。
核心方法
使用紫外-可见光谱和傅里叶变换红外(FTIR)光谱对合成的银纳米颗粒的尺寸、形状和稳定性进行了表征。
主要结果
生物合成的银纳米颗粒对两种蚊子均表现出很强的杀幼虫活性,根据剂量方法,白纹伊蚊的幼虫死亡率低于埃及伊蚊。

Evaluation of Escherichia coli-derived Silver Nanoparticles for Aedes Vector Control in Southern India

Authors: Rajalakshmi Anbalagan, Mortein Henri, M. Priyadharshini, Farhat Syed Kaseem, Sathya Jeevitha, Balachandar Vellingiri, Anand Setty Balakrishnan, Neena Elezebeth Philip, Mansi Malik, Sulochana Shekhar
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus are the principal mosquito vectors in Tamil Nadu transmitting the major arboviral diseases such as Dengue, Chikungunya, Zika and Yellow Fever. Aedes aegypti serves as the primary vector, followed by Aedes albopictus. Due to the limitations and environmental concerns associated with predictable chemical larvicides, there is an emergency need for alternative and sustainable vector control strategies. METHODS: In this study, environmentally friendly silver nanoparticles were synthesized using Escherichia coli through a biogenic approach that develops the bacterium's naturally reducing and stabilizing properties. The synthesized silver nanoparticles were characterized for size, shape, and stability using UV-Visible spectroscopy and Fourier Transform Infrared (FTIR) spectroscopy. The larvicidal efficacy of silver nanoparticles was evaluated against the larvae of Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus from the Thiruvarur district of Tamil Nadu, India. LC50 and LC90 values were estimated using probit analysis according to Finney's method (Miller-Tainter approach) in SPSS. RESULTS: The biosynthesized silver nanoparticles showed strong larvicidal activity against both mosquito species, depending on the dose approach, the moderate larval mortality was observed in Aedes albopictus than in Aedes aegypti. The median lethal concentration (LC50) was estimated at 270 ± 16 mg/L for Aedes aegypti and 194 ± 13 mg/L for Aedes albopictus, and LC90 values were estimated at 3480 ± 490 mg/L for Aedes aegypti and 2510 ± 360 mg/L for Aedes albopictus. The findings indicate that the biogenically synthesized silver nanoparticles are an effective alternative to chemical larvicides, offering enhanced efficacy with reduced environment impact. CONCLUSION: The current study highlights the dual advantages of utilizing biological resources for nanoparticle synthesis and implementing sustainable approaches for the control of mosquito- borne diseases. Future research should focus on large-scale production, field-level efficacy assessments, and evaluation of potential effects on non-target organisms to support practical applications.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

儿科登革热未解决的问题:从疾病机制到预防

Unresolved questions in pediatric dengue: from disease mechanisms to prevention
作者： Eduardo Lopez-Medina, Eduardo López-Medina
摘要中文翻译综述目的:尽管最近在了解登革热感染方面取得了进展,但在其流行病学、发病机制、诊断和预防(包括疫苗开发及其长期影响)方面仍存在很大差距。这篇综述总结了最近的研究结果,并强调了推进该领域并减轻个人和公共卫生负担的最紧迫的研究需求。最新发现:登革热负担有所增加,特别是在儿童和青少年中,并且已扩展到以前未受影响的地区。尽管如此,由于监测系统欠佳,当前数据可能低估了真正的负担。改善控制需要更好地了解严重登革热的发病机制,其中仍存在关键差距,同时在增加疫苗供应的情况下重新评估诊断局限性。疫苗是一种重要的补充工具,有几种候选疫苗正在研究中,两种目前正在使用,每种疫苗都有独特的优势、局限性和未解决的问题。摘要:需要更广泛的研究和投资来解决登革热日益严重的负担,包括严重的爆发和地理传播。与此同时,加强监管参与创新治疗和预防措施的制定和评估至关重要,特别是在负担最重的低收入和中等收入地区。关于登革热及其影响的清晰沟通和教育,以及经证明具有良好耐受性和有效性的可用预防措施,对于加强信任、提高吸收率和最大限度地提高公共卫生影响也至关重要。

Unresolved questions in pediatric dengue: from disease mechanisms to prevention

Authors: Eduardo Lopez-Medina, Eduardo López-Medina
Original Abstract
PURPOSE OF REVIEW: Despite recent advances in understanding dengue infection, substantial gaps remain in its epidemiology, pathogenesis, diagnosis, and prevention, including vaccine development and its long-term impact. This review summarizes recent findings and highlights the most pressing research needs to advance the field and reduce individual and public health burden. RECENT FINDINGS: Dengue burden has increased, particularly among children and adolescents, and it has expanded into previously unaffected regions. Still, current data likely underestimates the true burden due to suboptimal surveillance systems. Improving control will require a better understanding of severe dengue pathogenesis, where key gaps remain, alongside reassessment of diagnostic limitations in the context of increasing vaccine availability. Vaccines represent an important complementary tool, with several candidates under study and two currently in use, each with distinct strengths, limitations, and unresolved questions. SUMMARY: More extensive research and investment are needed to address the growing burden of dengue, including severe outbreaks and geographic spread. Meanwhile, stronger regulatory engagement in the development and evaluation of innovative therapeutic and preventive measures is essential, particularly in low-income and middle-income regions that bear the highest burden. Clear communication and education on dengue, its impact, and available preventive measures proven to be well tolerated and effective are also critical to strengthen trust, improve uptake, and maximize public health impact.

尼泊尔中部高海拔地区登革热负担上升:来自 2019-2023 年基于人群的纵向血清调查的证据

Rising dengue burden in high-altitude central Nepal: evidence from a population-based longitudinal serosurvey, 2019-2023
作者： Aastha Shrestha, Melina Thapa, Sony Shrestha, Sudichhya Tamrakar, Urursha Ranjitkar, Nishan Katuwal, Sabin Bikram Shahi, Shiva Ram Naga, Jason R. Andrews, Rajeev Shrestha
摘要中文翻译背景:由于气候变化、城市化和土地利用变化,登革热在全球范围内加剧。在尼泊尔,登革热已从低地地区扩展到高海拔地区,并于 2022 年和 2023 年爆发创纪录的疫情。然而,依赖被动监测和医院研究可能会低估社区层面的感染负担。方法 我们在尼泊尔加德满都和 Kavrepalanchok 地区开展了一项基于人群的血清学队列研究,从预先确定的医院服务区域招募了具有地理代表性、年龄分层的 0 至 25 岁居民随机样本。入组分两个阶段进行:第一阶段(2019 年 2 月至 2021 年 4 月),在约 3、6 和 12 个月进行随访;第二阶段(2023 年 2 月至 2023 年 6 月)重新访问原始队列。每次入户访问时,我们通过手指刺破滤纸收集毛细血管血样,并使用 InBios DENV DetectTM ELISA 测试样本针对登革热衍生重组抗原的 IgG 反应。使用制造商推荐的免疫状态比 (ISR) 临界值对血清状态进行分类。我们计算了每个时间点的血清阳性率,并通过识别每 1,000 人年的血清转化事件来估计血清发病率。我们使用多变量回归模型评估风险因素。结果 2019 年至 2023 年间,我们招募了 840 名参与者并收集了 2,082 份血液样本。总体血清发病率为每 1,000 人年 33.8 例(95% CI [24.9 - 45.0]),加德满都市区的发病率最高([105.7],95% CI [75.1 - 144.4])。血清发病率随着年龄和时间的推移而增加,从 2019 年的 46.1 增加到 2023 年的 51.0。与登革热阳性个体生活在同一家庭的参与者(调整后的 RR [4.65],95% CI [2.72 至 8.0])和有充满水的花盆的家庭(调整后的 RR [2.53],95% CI [1.28 至 5.74])血清转化的风险明显较高。结论 这项研究揭示了 2019 年至 2023 年间加德满都谷地登革热感染负担显着且不断增加。强调迫切需要立即采取公共卫生干预措施,以减缓尼泊尔高海拔地区登革热的上升。

证据基础与方法
入组分两个阶段进行:第一阶段(2019 年 2 月至 2021 年 4 月),在约 3、6 和 12 个月进行随访;第二阶段(2023 年 2 月至 2023 年 6 月)重新访问原始队列。
共识与结论
血清发病率随着年龄和时间的推移而增加,从 2019 年的 46.1 增加到 2023 年的 51.0。

所提供的证据没有明确指出争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
强调迫切需要立即采取公共卫生干预措施,以减缓尼泊尔高海拔地区登革热的蔓延。

Rising dengue burden in high-altitude central Nepal: evidence from a population-based longitudinal serosurvey, 2019-2023

Authors: Aastha Shrestha, Melina Thapa, Sony Shrestha, Sudichhya Tamrakar, Urursha Ranjitkar, Nishan Katuwal, Sabin Bikram Shahi, Shiva Ram Naga, Jason R. Andrews, Rajeev Shrestha
Original Abstract
Background: Dengue is intensifying globally due to climate change, urbanization, and land use changes. In Nepal, dengue has expanded from lowland regions to higher altitudes, with record outbreaks in 2022 and 2023. However, reliance on passive surveillance and hospital-based studies may underestimate community level infection burden. Methods We conducted a population-based serologic cohort study in Kathmandu and Kavrepalanchok districts, Nepal, enrolling a geographically representative, age stratified random sample of residents aged 0 to 25 years from pre-defined hospital catchment areas. Enrollment occurred in two phases: Phase I (February 2019 to April 2021) with follow-up visits at approximately 3, 6, and 12 months, and Phase II (February to June 2023) revisiting the original cohort. At each household visit, we collected capillary blood samples by finger-prick onto filter paper and tested the samples for IgG responses against dengue-derived recombinant antigen using InBios DENV DetectTM ELISA. Serostatus was classified using the manufacturer's recommended immune status ratio (ISR) cutoffs. We calculated seroprevalence at each time point and estimated seroincidence rates by identifying seroconversion events per 1,000 person-years. We assessed risk factors using multivariable regression models. Results Between 2019 and 2023, we enrolled 840 participants and collected 2,082 blood samples. The overall seroincidence rate was 33.8 per 1,000 person-years (95% CI [24.9 to 45.0]), with the highest rates in urban Kathmandu ([105.7], 95% CI [75.1 to 144.4]). Seroincidence increased with age and over time from 46.1 in 2019 to 51.0 in 2023. Participants living with a dengue-positive individual in the same household (adjusted RR [4.65], 95% CI [2.72 to 8.0]) and households with water-filled flower basins (adjusted RR [2.53], 95% CI [1.28 to 5.74]) had significantly higher risk of seroconversion. Conclusions This study reveals a significant and increasing burden of dengue infection in the Kathmandu Valley between 2019 and 2023. highlighting an urgent need for immediate public health interventions to mitigate dengue's rise in Nepal's higher-altitude regions.

Evidence base and method
Enrollment occurred in two phases: Phase I (February 2019 to April 2021) with follow-up visits at approximately 3, 6, and 12 months, and Phase II (February to June 2023) revisiting the original cohort.
Consensus and conclusions
Seroincidence increased with age and over time from 46.1 in 2019 to 51.0 in 2023.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
highlighting an urgent need for immediate public health interventions to mitigate dengue's rise in Nepal's higher-altitude regions.

气候变化和布尼亚病毒日益严重的威胁

Climate change and the growing threat of bunyaviruses
作者： James M. Bowen, Natasha L. Tilston, Natasha L. Tilston‐Lunel
摘要中文翻译摘要 三十多年来,人为气候变化及其对传染病的影响一直是科学和公共卫生讨论的核心。2026 年,我们现已进入气候适应阶段,越来越多的证据表明,持续的气候变化正在直接重塑包括布尼亚病毒在内的多种媒介传播病毒的动态。布尼亚病毒由多种分段、负义 RNA 病毒组成,其中包括重要的人类和兽医病原体,例如裂谷热病毒、克里米亚-刚果出血热病毒、奥罗普切病毒和卡什谷热病毒。尽管布尼亚病毒在医学和兽医学上具有重要意义,但与登革热、寨卡病毒、基孔肯雅热和黄热病等病毒相比,气候与病毒之间的关系仍然不太清楚。鉴于最近的流行病学变化,这一差距日益显着,例如 2022 年至 2025 年奥罗普切病毒在美洲和加勒比地区的扩张,这凸显了布尼亚病毒如何利用生态破坏,并强调需要更好地了解其传播的环境决定因素。基因组监测、实验病毒学和气候流行病学的进步现在使这些相互关联的病毒-宿主-载体-环境动态能够得到越来越精确的检查。在这篇小型综述中,我们综合了将环境变化与布尼亚病毒传播和地理扩张联系起来的现有证据,确定了关键的机制和监测差距,并概述了开发预测框架的优先事项,以在“同一个健康”背景下加强布尼亚病毒的防范。

提供的证据没有明确说明审查方法、数据库、资格流程或证据基础。

Climate change and the growing threat of bunyaviruses

Authors: James M. Bowen, Natasha L. Tilston, Natasha L. Tilston‐Lunel
Original Abstract
ABSTRACT Anthropogenic climate change and its implications for infectious diseases have been central to the scientific and public health discourse for more than three decades. In 2026, we have now entered a climate adaptation phase, with accumulating evidence demonstrating that ongoing climatic variability is directly reshaping the dynamics of multiple vector-borne viruses, including bunyaviruses. Bunyaviruses comprise a diverse group of segmented, negative-sense RNA viruses that include significant human and veterinary pathogens, such as Rift Valley fever virus, Crimean-Congo hemorrhagic fever virus, Oropouche virus, and Cache Valley fever virus. Despite their medical and veterinary importance, climate-virus relationships remain comparatively less well characterized for bunyaviruses than for viruses such as dengue, Zika, chikungunya, and yellow fever. This gap is increasingly notable in light of recent epidemiological shifts, exemplified by the 2022–2025 expansion of Oropouche virus across the Americas and the Caribbean, which highlights how bunyaviruses can exploit ecological disruption and underscores the need to better understand the environmental determinants of their transmission. Advances in genomic surveillance, experimental virology, and climate-informed epidemiology now enable these interconnected virus-host-vector-environment dynamics to be examined with increasing precision. In this minireview, we synthesize current evidence linking environmental change to bunyavirus transmission and geographic expansion, identify critical mechanistic and surveillance gaps, and outline priorities for developing predictive frameworks to strengthen bunyavirus preparedness within a One Health context.

Controversies and gaps
This gap is increasingly notable in light of recent epidemiological shifts, exemplified by the 2022–2025 expansion of Oropouche virus across the Americas and the Caribbean, which highlights how bunyaviruses can exploit ecological disruption and underscores the need to better understand the environmental determinants of their transmission.
Research and practice implications
In this minireview, we synthesize current evidence linking environmental change to bunyavirus transmission and geographic expansion, identify critical mechanistic and surveillance gaps, and outline priorities for developing predictive frameworks to strengthen bunyavirus preparedness within a One Health context.

奥罗普切病毒的死灰复燃:进化、流行病学、病理生理学、疫苗开发和全球卫生准备的回顾

The Resurgence of Oropouche Virus: A Review on Evolution, Epidemiology, Pathophysiology, Vaccine Development and Global Health Preparedness
作者： Aditya Agrawal, Praggaya Priya Lakra, Anil Gattani, Shailesh Kumar Patel, Rohini Gupta, Sriti Pandey, Ankush Kiran Niranjan, Ashok Kumar, Komal, Kumar Govil
摘要中文翻译奥罗普切病毒 (OROV) 是一种新兴的虫媒病毒,属于周布尼亚病毒科正布尼亚病毒属,于 1955 年首次在特立尼达和多巴哥发现,并在中美洲和南美洲重新成为严重的公共卫生问题。最近,随着 2024 年疫情爆发,OROV 病例数量不断增加,在流行地区和新受影响地区记录了超过 12,000 例病例,其中包括加勒比国家的首次发现病例以及美国、加拿大和意大利的输入病例。OROV 已在全球造成超过 500,000 例病例,由于与寨卡、基孔肯雅热和登革热的临床相似性,大量漏报病例。该病毒主要通过蠓传播。帕拉库蠓 (Goeldi),但也已鉴定出其他媒介,如库蚊 (Culex quinquefasciatus Say) 和锯缘伊蚊 (Theobald)。临床表现包括约 60% 的病例出现双相发热的急性发热性疾病,以及严重并发症,包括出血症状以及脑膜炎、脑炎和格林巴利综合征等神经系统后遗症。该病毒表现出复杂的传播周期(城市和森林)和重配驱动的进化。垂直传播的证据已经出现,有记录的胎儿小头畸形、死产和先天畸形的病例。发病机制涉及宿主先天免疫的调节,特别是干扰素反应。该病毒通过血脑屏障渗透表现出神经侵袭潜力,并已被证明可以在多种组织中持续存在。诊断挑战源于非特异性临床体征和专业实验室检测的有限性。目前,尚无针对 OROV 的具体治疗方法或疫苗,治疗仅限于支持性护理。然而,有前途的候选疫苗,如减毒活疫苗、灭活疫苗和基于重组蛋白的平台正在接受临床前和临床评估,为未来针对这种被忽视的热带疾病的预防策略带来希望。

发病机制涉及宿主先天免疫的调节,特别是干扰素反应。

科研与实践启示

The Resurgence of Oropouche Virus: A Review on Evolution, Epidemiology, Pathophysiology, Vaccine Development and Global Health Preparedness

Authors: Aditya Agrawal, Praggaya Priya Lakra, Anil Gattani, Shailesh Kumar Patel, Rohini Gupta, Sriti Pandey, Ankush Kiran Niranjan, Ashok Kumar, Komal, Kumar Govil
Original Abstract
The Oropouche virus (OROV) is an emerging arbovirus grouped under Orthobunyavirus genus of Peribunyaviridae family, first identified in Trinidad and Tobago in 1955, and re-emerged as a serious public health concern in the Central and South Americas. Recently, numbers of OROV cases are on the rise with the 2024 outbreak documenting over 12,000 cases across endemic and newly affected regions, including first-time detections in Caribbean nations and imported cases in the United States, Canada, and Italy. The OROV has caused over 5,00,000 cases globally, with substantial underreporting owing to clinical similarities with Zika, chikungunya and dengue. The virus is mainly transmitted by midges viz. Culicoides paraensis (Goeldi), though alternative vectors such as Culex quinquefasciatus Say and Aedes serratus (Theobald) have also been identified. Clinical manifestations range from acute febrile illness with biphasic fever in approximately 60% of cases to severe complications including haemorrhagic symptoms along with neurological sequelae such as meningitis, encephalitis, and Guillain-Barre syndrome. The virus exhibits complex transmission cycles (urban and sylvatic) and reassortment-driven evolution. The evidence of vertical transmission has emerged, with documented cases of fetal microcephaly, stillbirths, and congenital malformations. Pathogenesis involves modulation of host innate immunity, particularly interferon responses. The virus demonstrates neuroinvasive potential through blood-brain barrier penetration and has been shown to persist in various tissues. Diagnostic challenges arise from non-specific clinical signs and limited access to specialized laboratory tests. At present, no specific treatment or vaccines against the OROV are available and management limited to supportive care. However, promising vaccine candidates such as live-attenuated, inactivated, and recombinant protein-based platforms are undergoing preclinical and clinical evaluation, offering hope for future preventive strategies against this neglected tropical disease.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Clinical manifestations range from acute febrile illness with biphasic fever in approximately 60% of cases to severe complications including haemorrhagic symptoms along with neurological sequelae such as meningitis, encephalitis, and Guillain-Barre syndrome.
Evidence base and method
The OROV has caused over 5,00,000 cases globally, with substantial underreporting owing to clinical similarities with Zika, chikungunya and dengue.
Consensus and conclusions
Pathogenesis involves modulation of host innate immunity, particularly interferon responses.
Controversies and gaps
Diagnostic challenges arise from non-specific clinical signs and limited access to specialized laboratory tests.
Research and practice implications
At present, no specific treatment or vaccines against the OROV are available and management limited to supportive care.

蚊子的杀虫剂抗药性:可持续病媒控制的现状、机制、监测和创新策略

Insecticide resistance in mosquitoes: Current status, mechanism, monitoring and innovative strategies for sustainable vector control
作者： Divya Kataria, Sangeeta Janjoter, Nisha Dahiya, Mahima Yadav, Ritu Yadav, Vaishali Verma, Neelam Sehrawat
摘要中文翻译疟疾、登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒和黄热病等蚊媒疾病仍然是全世界主要的公共卫生挑战,每年造成数百万例病例和数千人死亡。病媒控制一直是预防策略的基石,其中杀虫剂是最常用的干预措施。然而,杀虫剂的广泛且往往不加区别的使用导致蚊子种群出现杀虫剂抗药性,从而威胁到现有控制措施的可持续性。本综述介绍了杀虫剂耐药性的现状、其潜在机制以及全球耐药性趋势。还强调了替代病媒控制策略,例如基因改造、基于沃尔巴克氏体的方法、植物杀虫剂和纳米技术驱动的控制措施。它还探讨了杀虫剂抗性监测、综合抗性管理策略的重要性,以及对具有独特作用模式的新化合物的需求,以确保病媒控制的持续有效性。

Insecticide resistance in mosquitoes: Current status, mechanism, monitoring and innovative strategies for sustainable vector control

Authors: Divya Kataria, Sangeeta Janjoter, Nisha Dahiya, Mahima Yadav, Ritu Yadav, Vaishali Verma, Neelam Sehrawat
Original Abstract
Mosquito-borne diseases such as malaria, dengue, chikungunya, Zika and yellow fever remain major public health challenges worldwide, causing millions of cases and thousands of deaths annually. Vector control has been the cornerstone of prevention strategies, with insecticides being the most commonly used intervention. However, the widespread and often indiscriminate use of insecticides has led to the emergence of insecticide resistance in the mosquito population, thereby threatening the sustainability of existing control measures. This review presents the current status of insecticide resistance, its underlying mechanisms, and global trends in resistance. The alternative vector control strategies, such as genetic modification, Wolbachia-based methods, botanical insecticides and nanotechnology-driven control measures, are also highlighted. It also explores the significance of insecticide resistance monitoring, integrated resistance management strategies and the need for new compounds with a distinct mode of action to ensure the continued effectiveness of vector control.

Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
It also explores the significance of insecticide resistance monitoring, integrated resistance management strategies and the need for new compounds with a distinct mode of action to ensure the continued effectiveness of vector control.

🚨 突发动态与新闻 / Health News

Published: 2026-07-16  |  Publisher: ReliefWeb
A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

萨摩亚登革热疫情应对 - DREF 最终报告 (MDRWS003) - ReliefWeb

Samoa Dengue Outbreak Response - DREF Final Report (MDRWS003) - ReliefWeb
新闻简报萨摩亚卫生部于2025年4月17日宣布登革热疫情暴发,病例在8月中旬达到峰值,单周新增诊断病例超过2,000例。截至报告期末(流行病学第34周),累计报告临床诊断病例10,982例、实验室确诊病例3,379例,其中包括6例死亡病例,死亡病例以儿童为主。疫情对15岁以下儿童影响尤为严重,确诊病例中72%为该年龄段患者,且主要集中在乌波卢岛,特别是阿皮亚城市区和西北乌波卢地区。血清型检测显示,91%为登革病毒1型(DENV-1),9%为登革病毒2型(DENV-2)。截至8月下旬,病例数呈现周环比下降19%的趋势,但由于病毒仍在持续传播,蓝色警戒仍然生效。约20%的病例需要住院治疗,未报告重症监护病例。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026 年 7 月 16 日。事件日期:2025 年 4 月 17 日宣布爆发;2025 年 8 月 5 日触发;2025 年 8 月中旬达到峰值(第 32 周);报告期截至第 34 周。
地点与对象
萨摩亚,主要是乌波卢岛(88%的病例),集中在阿皮亚市区和乌波卢岛西北部;法莱阿塔区占病例的 32%。受影响人群多为 15 岁以下人群(占确诊病例的 72%)。
事件
卫生部于 2025 年 4 月 17 日正式宣布登革热疫情。该事件已确认为疫情,出现实验室确诊病例和死亡病例。IFRC 将回应记录在 DREF 最终报告 (MDRWS003) 中。
规模、影响与风险
累计临床诊断病例:10,982例(实验室确诊病例3,379例)。已确认六例与登革热相关的死亡病例,其中包括儿童。大约20%的病例需要住院治疗;没有 ICU 入院报告。发病率高峰:每 100,000 人中有 1,108 例(发病后第 30 周)。血清型:91% DENV-1、9% DENV-2。风险:尽管到第 34 周病例每周减少 19%,但蓝色警报表明病毒仍在继续传播。
应对、状态与不确定性
红十字与红新月联会和卫生部进行了监测、住院和治疗。到第 34 周,观察到持续下降趋势,但由于持续流通,蓝色警报仍然有效。提供的证据中没有报告有关接触者追踪或病媒控制措施的进一步细节。最终报告于2026年7月15日发布,并于2026年7月16日发布在ReliefWeb上。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Samoa Dengue Outbreak Response - DREF Final Report (MDRWS003) - ReliefWeb

News Brief
Samoa’s Ministry of Health declared a dengue fever outbreak on 17 April 2025, with cases peaking in mid-August at over 2,000 new diagnoses in a single week. By the end of the reporting period (epi-week 34), 10,982 clinically diagnosed cases and 3,379 laboratory-confirmed cases had been recorded, including six fatalities, predominantly children. The outbreak disproportionately affected children under 15 years old, who accounted for 72% of confirmed cases, and was concentrated in Upolu Island, particularly in Apia Urban Area and Northwest Upolu. Serotyping showed 91% DENV-1 and 9% DENV-2. While a 19% weekly decline in cases was observed by late August, a Blue Alert remained in effect due to ongoing viral circulation. Approximately 20% of cases required hospitalization, with no intensive care admissions reported.
View five news elements
Time
Publication date: 16 July 2026. Event date: Outbreak declared 17 April 2025; trigger met 5 August 2025; peak in mid-August 2025 (epi-week 32); reporting period ended by epi-week 34.
Location and population
Samoa, primarily Upolu Island (88% of cases), with concentration in Apia Urban Area and Northwest Upolu; Faleata District accounted for 32% of cases. Affected population skewed toward individuals under 15 years old (72% of confirmed cases).
Event
The Ministry of Health officially declared a dengue fever outbreak on 17 April 2025. The event is confirmed as an outbreak with laboratory-confirmed cases and fatalities. The response was documented in a DREF Final Report (MDRWS003) by the IFRC.
Scale, impact and risk
Cumulative clinically diagnosed cases: 10,982 (3,379 laboratory-confirmed). Six dengue-related fatalities confirmed, including children. Approximately 20% of cases required hospitalization; no ICU admissions reported. Peak incidence rate: 1,108 cases per 100,000 population (epi-week 30). Serotypes: 91% DENV-1, 9% DENV-2. Risk: Ongoing viral circulation indicated by a Blue Alert despite a 19% weekly decrease in cases by epi-week 34.
Response, status and uncertainty
The IFRC and Ministry of Health conducted surveillance, hospitalization, and treatment. A sustained downward trend was observed by epi-week 34, but a Blue Alert remained in effect due to ongoing circulation. No further details on contact tracing or vector control measures were reported in the supplied evidence. The final report was published on 15 July 2026 and posted on ReliefWeb on 16 July 2026.

日本制药商武田(Takeda)的 Qdenga 成为印度批准的首个登革热疫苗

Japanese drugmaker Takeda's Qdenga becomes first dengue vaccine approved in India
新闻简报On July 20, 2026, India's drug regulator approved Takeda Pharmaceutical's QDENGA vaccine, marking it as the first dengue vaccine approved in the country. This tetravalent live-attenuated vaccine is designed to protect against all four dengue virus serotypes and can be administered without prior dengue exposure testing. The approval comes at a critical time, as reported dengue cases in India have surged 11-fold over the past two decades. India bears nearly one-third of the global dengue burden, making this approval significant for public health. Takeda has partnered with Indian vaccine maker Biological E to expand production, aiming to manufacture 100 million doses globally by the end of the decade. Since its launch in 2022, QDENGA has been approved in 43 countries, with over 32 million doses distributed worldwide.（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
活动日期为2026-07-20,发布日期为2026-07-21。
地点与对象
地点是印度。受影响的人群是普通公众,特别是那些有登革热风险的人群。
事件
此次事件是武田制药的登革热QDENGA疫苗获得印度药品监管机构的批准,使其成为印度批准的首个登革热疫苗。这是该公司报告的已确认事件。
规模、影响与风险
过去 20 年来,印度报告的登革热病例增加了 11 倍。该疫苗旨在预防所有四种登革热病毒血清型,不需要接种前测试。印度占全球登革热负担的近三分之一。
应对、状态与不确定性
Takeda partnered with Indian vaccine maker Biological E in 2024 to expand manufacturing of QDENGA. Biological E plans to build capacity to produce 50 million doses annually, supporting Takeda's goal of manufacturing 100 million doses a year globally by the end of the decade. The vaccine has been approved in 43 countries, with more than 32 million doses distributed globally since its launch in 2022.

DCGI 批准武田 QDENGA,印度获得首个登革热疫苗

India gets first dengue vaccine as DCGI approves Takeda’s QDENGA
新闻简报India’s drug regulator has granted approval to Takeda’s QDENGA, marking the country’s first licensed dengue vaccine as cases surge 11-fold over the past two decades. The tetravalent live-attenuated vaccine protects against all four dengue virus serotypes and can be administered without prior infection testing, simplifying deployment. With India bearing nearly one-third of the global dengue burden, the approval arrives amid escalating seasonal outbreaks that strain public health systems. Takeda is scaling local production through a partnership with Biological E, targeting 50 million doses annually in India as part of a global 100-million-dose capacity plan by decade’s end. Wider rollout hinges on pricing, supply logistics, and national immunization policy decisions, though experts view the vaccine as a critical addition to existing mosquito-control and awareness measures.（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026 年 7 月 20 日 13:31(美国标准时间);“星期一”(2026 年 7 月 20 日)之后未指定监管批准事件日期。
地点与对象
印度(国家);疫苗适用于有登革热暴露风险的普通人群。
事件
印度药品监管机构批准了武田的 QDENGA,这是该国首款登革热疫苗,作为预防登革热病毒感染的工具。
规模、影响与风险
过去 20 年来,印度的登革热病例增加了 11 倍;印度占全球登革热负担的近三分之一。证据中没有提供具体病例或死亡人数。
应对、状态与不确定性
Regulatory approval granted; wider rollout pending pricing, supply, national immunization recommendations, and integration into prevention strategies. Takeda is expanding manufacturing in India via a partnership with Biological E to produce up to 50 million doses annually locally, contributing to a global target of 100 million doses per year by the end of the decade. QDENGA is a tetravalent live-attenuated vaccine protecting against all four dengue serotypes, suitable for individuals regardless of prior dengue infection, and does not require pre-vaccination testing.
