印度批准首个针对 4 至 60 岁人群的登革热疫苗

India approves first dengue vaccine for individuals aged 4 to 60 years
新闻简报印度药品监管机构印度药品监管总局 (DCGI) 已批准武田的 QDENGA (TAK-003) 作为该国首个登革热疫苗,批准其用于 4 至 60 岁的个人。这种减毒活四价疫苗分两剂接种,间隔三个月,针对所有四种登革热病毒血清型,无需事先进行感染筛查。该批准是在一项涉及 19 项试验的 28,000 多名参与者参与的全球临床计划之后获得的,其中包括一项在八个登革热流行国家有 20,000 多名参与者参与的关键 III 期研究。武田报告称,在第二次给药后 12 个月时,对经病毒学证实的登革热有 80.2% 的疗效,在 18 个月时对住院治疗的疗效为 90.4%,持续保护作用长达七年。世界卫生组织推荐该疫苗用于传播率较高的登革热流行地区,并已对其进行了资格预审。武田计划与印度当局合作以促进推广。

时间
发布于 2026 年 7 月 20 日 16:18 IST(证据中未指定事件日期)。

事件

规模、影响与风险
所提供的证据中未报告。
应对、状态与不确定性
疫苗是一种减毒活四价两次剂量方案(剂量间隔三个月),旨在预防所有四种登革热病毒血清型,并且无论先前是否感染过登革热,都可以接种,无需进行疫苗接种前筛查。批准基于全球临床开发计划,涉及 19 项试验,超过 28,000 名参与者;关键的 III 期试验招募了来自八个登革热流行国家的 20,000 多名参与者。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

India approves first dengue vaccine for individuals aged 4 to 60 years

News Brief
India’s drug regulator, the Drug Controller General of India (DCGI), has approved Takeda’s QDENGA (TAK-003) as the country’s first dengue vaccine, clearing it for use in individuals aged 4 to 60 years. The live-attenuated, tetravalent vaccine is administered in two doses three months apart and targets all four dengue virus serotypes without requiring prior infection screening. Approval follows a global clinical programme involving more than 28,000 participants across 19 trials, including a pivotal Phase III study with over 20,000 participants in eight dengue-endemic countries. Takeda reports 80.2% efficacy against virologically confirmed dengue at 12 months post-second dose and 90.4% efficacy against hospitalisation at 18 months, with sustained protection demonstrated up to seven years. The World Health Organization recommends the vaccine for dengue-endemic settings with high transmission and has prequalified it. Takeda plans to work with Indian authorities to facilitate rollout.
View five news elements
Time
Published on 20 July 2026 at 16:18 IST (event date not specified in evidence).
Location and population
India (national approval); vaccine approved for individuals aged 4 to 60 years.
Event
The Drug Controller General of India (DCGI) granted market authorisation to Takeda Biopharmaceuticals India’s QDENGA (TAK-003), the first dengue vaccine approved in India, as reported by The Hindu Bureau on 20 July 2026.
Scale, impact and risk
Not reported in the supplied evidence.
Response, status and uncertainty
Vaccine is a live-attenuated tetravalent two-dose regimen (doses three months apart), designed to protect against all four dengue virus serotypes and can be administered irrespective of prior dengue infection without pre-vaccination screening. Approval based on global clinical development programme with over 28,000 participants across 19 trials; pivotal Phase III trial enrolled over 20,000 participants across eight dengue-endemic countries.

新抗体有望缓解登革热

New antibody offers possible dengue fever relief
新闻简报A Taiwanese research team from the National Health Research Institutes and National Cheng Kung University has developed a bispecific antibody designed to block two immune receptors (CLEC5A and TLR2) implicated in severe dengue inflammation, offering a potential therapeutic approach for the disease. The antibody demonstrated efficacy in preclinical experiments against all four dengue virus serotypes and certain severe-disease-associated strains. Globally, dengue causes an estimated 390 million infections annually, with 500,000 progressing to severe forms and 22,000 deaths. In Taiwan, the largest recent outbreak in 2015 resulted in 43,000 cases and 228 deaths. Separately, Chiayi City confirmed its first imported dengue case of the year in a student returning from Vietnam, prompting local health authorities to conduct mosquito control measures and public health advisories. While the antibody represents a promising advance, further clinical validation is required before potential deployment.（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表于2026-07-20;报告中未指定活动日期(2026年7月20日新闻发布会上公布的研究结果)。
地点与对象
台湾台北(卫生福利部);由国立卫生研究院 (NHRI) 和国立成功大学 (NCKU) 进行的研究;台湾嘉义市(输入性登革热病例)。
事件
NHRI 和 NCKU 的研究小组报告了一种针对 CLEC5A 和 TLR2 受体的双特异性抗体的开发,可抑制登革热病毒感染中的严重炎症反应,显示出治疗严重登革热的潜力。另外,嘉义市政府卫生局证实一宗输入性登革热个案,涉及一名从越南回国的学生。
规模、影响与风险
Global: ~390 million dengue infections annually, ~96 million symptomatic cases, ~500,000 severe cases, ~22,000 deaths. Taiwan: 43,000 confirmed cases and 228 deaths in 2015 (largest outbreak in decades). The imported case in Chiayi City involved a student with fever, headache, and pain behind the eyes, testing positive for dengue fever. No vaccine exists for effective prevention; treatment relies on supportive care. The bispecific antibody showed efficacy against all four dengue virus serotypes and some severe-disease-associated strains in experiments.
应对、状态与不确定性
研究结果发表在《生物医学科学杂志》上。NHRI 和 NCKU 研究人员强调需要进行进一步的临床试验来验证双特异性抗体在人体中的安全性和有效性。对于输入性病例,嘉义市政府卫生局采取了灭蚊措施(患者家周围100m范围内喷洒喷雾、蚊子密度调查、清除孳生地),并建议居民清除积水并采取防蚊措施。该抗体对寨卡病毒和日本脑炎的潜在适用性仍停留在理论上。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

New antibody offers possible dengue fever relief

News Brief
A Taiwanese research team from the National Health Research Institutes and National Cheng Kung University has developed a bispecific antibody designed to block two immune receptors (CLEC5A and TLR2) implicated in severe dengue inflammation, offering a potential therapeutic approach for the disease. The antibody demonstrated efficacy in preclinical experiments against all four dengue virus serotypes and certain severe-disease-associated strains. Globally, dengue causes an estimated 390 million infections annually, with 500,000 progressing to severe forms and 22,000 deaths. In Taiwan, the largest recent outbreak in 2015 resulted in 43,000 cases and 228 deaths. Separately, Chiayi City confirmed its first imported dengue case of the year in a student returning from Vietnam, prompting local health authorities to conduct mosquito control measures and public health advisories. While the antibody represents a promising advance, further clinical validation is required before potential deployment.
View five news elements
Time
Published on 2026-07-20; event date not specified in the report (research findings presented at a news conference on 2026-07-20).
Location and population
Taipei, Taiwan (Ministry of Health and Welfare); research conducted by National Health Research Institutes (NHRI) and National Cheng Kung University (NCKU); Chiayi City, Taiwan (imported dengue case).
Event
A research team from NHRI and NCKU reported the development of a bispecific antibody targeting CLEC5A and TLR2 receptors to suppress severe inflammatory responses in dengue virus infection, showing potential as a treatment for severe dengue fever. Separately, the Chiayi City Government Health Bureau confirmed an imported dengue fever case involving a student returning from Vietnam.
Scale, impact and risk
Global: ~390 million dengue infections annually, ~96 million symptomatic cases, ~500,000 severe cases, ~22,000 deaths. Taiwan: 43,000 confirmed cases and 228 deaths in 2015 (largest outbreak in decades). The imported case in Chiayi City involved a student with fever, headache, and pain behind the eyes, testing positive for dengue fever. No vaccine exists for effective prevention; treatment relies on supportive care. The bispecific antibody showed efficacy against all four dengue virus serotypes and some severe-disease-associated strains in experiments.
Response, status and uncertainty
The research findings were published in the Journal of Biomedical Science. NHRI and NCKU researchers emphasized the need for further clinical trials to validate the bispecific antibody's safety and efficacy in humans. For the imported case, the Chiayi City Government Health Bureau conducted mosquito control measures (spraying within 100m of the patient's home, mosquito density surveys, and breeding site removal) and advised residents to eliminate standing water and use mosquito bite precautions. The antibody's potential applicability to Zika virus and Japanese encephalitis remains theoretical.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

随着南亚病例激增,印度批准首款登革热疫苗

India approves first dengue vaccine as cases surge in South Asia
新闻简报India’s drug regulator has approved Takeda Pharmaceutical’s dengue vaccine QDENGA, marking the country’s first dengue vaccine amid a severe regional surge in cases. The approval follows a review of phase III trial data involving more than 20,000 participants across eight countries, including Sri Lanka, and 480 volunteers in India. Regulators concluded the vaccine is safe and immunogenic for adults and children aged 4–60 years. The move comes as South Asia faces escalating outbreaks: Sri Lanka reported 76,044 dengue cases and 53 deaths so far in 2026, while Bangladesh recorded over 9,000 cases and 30 deaths. India accounts for nearly one-third of the global dengue burden. Takeda plans to manufacture up to 50 million doses annually in India through a partnership with Biological E, supporting a global production target of 100 million doses per year by 2030. Regulators have mandated post-marketing safety studies within six months of launch, and experts stress the vaccine should complement existing strategies such as vector control and surveillance.（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026 年 7 月 20 日。在近年来报告病例激增期间,事件发生时间被称为“关键”,但证据中没有提供具体的事件日期。
地点与对象
南亚地区(斯里兰卡、孟加拉国)。受影响人群包括 4 至 60 岁的成人和儿童。病媒:伊蚊。
事件
印度药品监管机构药品标准控制中心(CDSCO)批准了武田制药的登革热疫苗 QDENGA。此次批准是在对第三阶段试验数据进行审查之后进行的,被描述为针对登革热负担上升的公共卫生反应。
规模、影响与风险
斯里兰卡:2026 年报告了 76,044 例登革热病例和 53 例死亡(截至报告日期)。孟加拉国:报告病例超过 9,000 例,死亡人数 30 人。印度约占全球登革热负担的三分之一。QDENGA 是一种减毒活四价疫苗,旨在预防所有四种登革热病毒血清型。死亡率相对较低,但疫情爆发给卫生系统带来了沉重负担。
应对、状态与不确定性
CDSCO directed Takeda to conduct post-marketing safety and effectiveness studies in India within six months of commercial launch. QDENGA is already approved in over 40 countries and WHO pre-qualified. Takeda is partnering with Biological E to manufacture up to 50 million doses annually in India, supporting a global production target of 100 million doses per year by the end of the decade. Experts emphasize vaccination should complement surveillance, vector control, and development of new treatments. Specific populations (e.g., pregnant women, newborns, older adults) are noted as not benefiting from this vaccine.

Event

Scale, impact and risk
Sri Lanka: 76,044 dengue cases and 53 deaths reported in 2026 (as of the report date). Bangladesh: over 9,000 cases and 30 deaths reported. India accounts for approximately one-third of the global dengue burden. QDENGA is a live-attenuated tetravalent vaccine designed to protect against all four dengue virus serotypes. Mortality is described as relatively low, but outbreaks impose substantial health-system burden.
Response, status and uncertainty
CDSCO directed Takeda to conduct post-marketing safety and effectiveness studies in India within six months of commercial launch. QDENGA is already approved in over 40 countries and WHO pre-qualified. Takeda is partnering with Biological E to manufacture up to 50 million doses annually in India, supporting a global production target of 100 million doses per year by the end of the decade. Experts emphasize vaccination should complement surveillance, vector control, and development of new treatments. Specific populations (e.g., pregnant women, newborns, older adults) are noted as not benefiting from this vaccine.

印度首个登革热疫苗获批:DCGI 批准武田 Qdenga 用于 4 至 60 岁人群

India’s first dengue vaccine approved: DCGI clears Takeda’s Qdenga for those aged 4 to 60
新闻简报India’s drug regulator, the Drug Controller General of India (DCGI), has granted the country’s first-ever approval for a dengue vaccine, Qdenga, developed by Takeda Biopharmaceuticals India. The two-dose vaccine, administered three months apart, is authorized for individuals aged 4 to 60 and protects against all four dengue virus serotypes without requiring pre-vaccination testing. The approval arrives amid a sharp rise in dengue cases in India, where reported infections have surged 11-fold over the past two decades and the country bears nearly one-third of the global dengue burden. Clinical trial data show sustained efficacy, with 80.2% protection against virologically confirmed dengue at 12 months and 84.1% efficacy in preventing hospitalizations after 4.5 years. Takeda has partnered with Biological E to produce up to 50 million doses annually, though pricing remains unannounced. The vaccine also holds WHO prequalification, facilitating potential procurement through international agencies.（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026-07-20(《印度快报》)。事件日期:DCGI于2026-07-20或之前批准(N3)。
地点与对象
批准用于 4 至 60 岁的个体,无论之前是否接触过登革热(N4、N19)。
事件
印度药品监管总局(DCGI)批准武田生物制药印度公司的登革热疫苗 Qdenga,标志着印度首个登革热疫苗授权(N3)。根据监管审查和临床数据(N4、N19)确认该疫苗可以使用。
规模、影响与风险
印度占全球登革热负担的近三分之一(N12、N14)。过去 20 年来,印度报告的登革热病例增加了 11 倍(N2、N11)。该疫苗针对所有四种登革热病毒血清型(DENV-1 至 DENV-4)(N4、N18)。临床试验表明,在第二次给药后 12 个月时,对病毒学确诊的登革热有 80.2% 的疗效 (N25),在 18 个月时对登革热相关住院的疗效为 90.4% (N26),在 4.5 年后预防登革热相关住院的疗效为 84.1% (N27)。该疫苗已显示出长达七年的持续安全性和有效性(N28)。
应对、状态与不确定性
The vaccine is administered as a two-dose regimen (0.5 mL each) three months apart (N7). It does not require pre-vaccination testing (N5). The price has not been announced; manufacturing partnership with Biological E aims for up to 50 million doses annually (N8). Pricing may depend on inclusion in government immunization programs (N9). The vaccine has WHO prequalification (N32). Long-term safety and efficacy data are supported by clinical trials and ongoing pharmacovigilance (N23, N24, N31).

What Is QDENGA? India Gets Its First Dengue Vaccine As Cases Continue To Rise; Know Availability, Benefits And - Free Press Journal

News Brief
India’s drug regulator has approved QDENGA (TAK-003), a live-attenuated dengue vaccine that targets all four dengue virus serotypes, marking the country’s first dengue vaccine. Developed by Takeda, the two-dose regimen is administered three months apart and can be given regardless of prior dengue infection, unlike earlier vaccines. Clinical studies indicate the vaccine lowers the risk of hospitalisation and severe dengue, including dengue haemorrhagic fever and shock syndrome, though it does not provide complete protection. While the Central Drugs Standard Control Organisation’s approval paves the way for use, the vaccine’s rollout hinges on manufacturing capacity, pricing, and distribution plans. Health authorities are expected to issue national recommendations on eligible age groups and vaccination protocols. Rising monsoon-driven transmission and urbanisation have intensified India’s dengue burden, making vaccination a key component alongside mosquito control in mitigating outbreaks.

DCGI 批准武田 QDENGA,印度首个登革热疫苗:内部详细信息 - WION

DCGI clears Takeda's QDENGA, India's first-ever dengue vaccine: Full details inside - WION
新闻简报On July 20, 2026, India's Drug Controller General of India (DCGI) approved Takeda Biopharmaceuticals India's dengue vaccine, QDENGA, marking the country's first-ever dengue vaccine authorization. The vaccine protects against all four dengue virus strains and is approved for individuals aged four to 60, regardless of prior dengue exposure, eliminating the need for pre-vaccination testing. Clinical data from extensive global trials, including the pivotal Phase III TIDES and DEN-302 trials involving over 20,000 participants, demonstrate high efficacy, with 80.2% protection against confirmed dengue at 12 months and 90.4% against hospitalization at 18 months. Long-term data shows sustained efficacy up to seven years. The vaccine is administered as a two-dose subcutaneous injection spaced three months apart. While pricing remains undetermined, Takeda's partnership with Biological E aims to produce up to 50 million doses annually, with affordability and potential inclusion in government immunization schemes influencing final pricing. WHO has also endorsed the vaccine for global standards compliance.（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布时间: 2026 年 7 月 20 日 22:03 IST |更新时间:2026 年 7 月 20 日,22:03(美国标准时间)。
地点与对象
印度;4 至 60 岁的人口,无论之前是否接触过登革热;可能纳入政府免疫计划。
事件
印度药品监管总局 (DCGI) 已批准武田生物制药印度公司的登革热疫苗 QDENGA,这标志着印度首次批准登革热疫苗。
规模、影响与风险
该疫苗可针对所有四种登革热病毒株(DENV-1、DENV-2、DENV-3、DENV-4)提供保护。第二次给药后 12 个月,针对经病毒学证实的登革热的疗效为 80.2%;18 个月时,针对登革热相关住院的有效率达 90.4%。4.5 年的长期数据显示,预防住院治疗的有效性为 84.1%,安全性和有效性可持续长达七年。
应对、状态与不确定性
The approval is confirmed by DCGI. WHO has also given a nod for the vaccine, confirming it meets global standards for quality, safety, and efficacy. Pricing has not been finalised yet but is expected to be affordable for Indians, given Takeda's partnership with Hyderabad-based Biological E to produce up to 50 million doses annually. The final price will also hinge on whether the vaccine is eventually included in government-run immunisation schemes. Ongoing real-world evidence and pharmacovigilance continue to reinforce these findings.

India approves dengue vaccine: Doctor explains the four virus strains and why immunity remains a major challenge
新闻简报印度已批准其首款登革热疫苗 QDENGA (TAK-003),由印度武田生物制药公司开发,用于 4 至 60 岁的个体。该疫苗分两剂接种,间隔三个月,旨在预防所有四种登革热病毒血清型(DENV-1 至 DENV-4),旨在减少严重登革热和住院治疗。公共卫生专家警告说,虽然疫苗提供了广泛的保护,但它并不能提供 100% 的免疫力,应辅之以驱蚊剂、防护服、蚊帐和消除积水等蚊虫叮咬预防措施。仅在咨询医疗保健专业人员并遵循国家指南后才建议接种疫苗,因为疫苗的有效性可能因血清型和既往感染状况而异。该公告是在认识到登革热的复杂性之后做出的,其中一种血清型的感染不能提供针对其他三种血清型的免疫力,并且重复感染可能会带来更高的严重疾病风险。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

India approves dengue vaccine: Doctor explains the four virus strains and why immunity remains a major challenge

News Brief
India has approved its first dengue vaccine, QDENGA (TAK-003), developed by Takeda Biopharmaceuticals India, for use in individuals aged 4 to 60 years. The vaccine, administered in two doses three months apart, is designed to protect against all four dengue virus serotypes (DENV-1 to DENV-4) and aims to reduce severe dengue and hospitalization. Public health experts caution that while the vaccine offers broad protection, it does not confer 100% immunity and should be complemented by mosquito-bite prevention measures such as repellents, protective clothing, mosquito nets, and eliminating stagnant water. Vaccination is advised only after consulting a healthcare professional and following national guidelines, as effectiveness may vary by serotype and prior infection status. The announcement follows recognition of dengue's complexity, where infection with one serotype does not provide immunity against the other three, and repeat infections can pose higher risks of severe disease.
View five news elements
Time
Published on 20 July 2026 at 23:11 IST (event date not specified in the evidence).
Location and population
India (national scope); vaccine approved for individuals aged 4 to 60 years.
Event
India's first dengue vaccine, QDENGA (TAK-003), developed by Takeda Biopharmaceuticals India, has been approved for use. The vaccine is reported to protect against all four dengue virus serotypes (DENV-1 to DENV-4) and is administered in two doses three months apart. The approval is presented as a public health measure to reduce severe dengue and hospitalization.
Scale, impact and risk
Not reported in the supplied evidence.
Response, status and uncertainty
Vaccination is recommended to follow national guidelines and should complement, not replace, mosquito-bite prevention measures. Experts emphasize that no vaccine provides 100% immunity and that vaccination should be taken only after consulting a qualified healthcare professional. The vaccine's effectiveness varies by serotype and prior infection status; incomplete protection may not be adequate. Prevention measures include repellents, clothing, nets, and eliminating mosquito breeding sites.

2 型糖尿病会增加严重登革热并发症的风险:综述

Type 2 diabetes raises risk of severe dengue complications: Review
新闻简报糖尿病专家的一篇评论论文显示,2 型糖尿病患者因登革热而出现严重、危及生命的并发症的风险明显更高。这篇论文由 Jothydev Kesavadev 博士领导,由欧洲国际糖尿病联合会候任主席 Niti Pall 博士等顶尖糖尿病专家共同撰写,并在科瓦兰举行的第 14 届 Jothydev 专业教育论坛年度全球糖尿病大会上发表。周日与媒体分享的研究结果强调,持续高血糖水平会损害免疫功能并损害血管,使糖尿病患者更容易受到严重登革热感染,包括登革出血热和登革休克综合症。作者建议将登革热疫苗接种纳入常规糖尿病护理中,以减少登革热流行地区的严重疾病和住院治疗。在登革热仍然是主要公共卫生挑战的地区,这种方法可以加强医疗保健服务并改善数百万糖尿病患者的健康状况。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该审查于 2026 年 7 月 19 日发表,研究结果于周日与媒体分享,并在科瓦兰举行的一次会议上发表了论文。
地点与对象
该审查重点关注登革热流行地区的 2 型糖尿病患者,特别是在印度和其他登革热构成重大公共卫生挑战的地区。
事件
糖尿病专家的一篇评论论文发现,2 型糖尿病患者面临严重、危及生命的登革热并发症的风险明显更高。该论文建议将登革热疫苗接种纳入常规糖尿病护理中。[N2、N3、N4、N10、N12]。
规模、影响与风险
该审查强调,持续高血糖会损害免疫功能并损害血管,使糖尿病患者更容易受到严重登革热感染,包括登革出血热和登革休克综合症。
应对、状态与不确定性
研究结果与媒体分享,并在第 14 届 Jothydev 职业教育论坛年度全球糖尿病大会上发表了论文。该审查建议将登革热疫苗接种纳入常规糖尿病护理中,以减少严重疾病和住院治疗。[N5、N7、N10、N12、N13]。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Type 2 diabetes raises risk of severe dengue complications: Review

News Brief
A review paper by diabetes specialists has revealed that people with Type 2 diabetes are at a significantly higher risk of severe, life-threatening complications from dengue fever. The paper, led by Dr Jothydev Kesavadev and co-authored by leading diabetes experts, including Dr Niti Pall, President-Elect of the International Diabetes Federation, Europe, was presented at the 14th Jothydev’s Professional Education Forum Annual Global Diabetes Convention in Kovalam. The findings, shared with the media on Sunday, highlight that persistently high blood sugar levels impair immune function and damage blood vessels, making individuals with diabetes more susceptible to severe dengue infections, including dengue haemorrhagic fever and dengue shock syndrome. The authors recommend integrating dengue vaccination into routine diabetes care to reduce severe illness and hospitalisation in dengue-endemic regions. This approach could strengthen healthcare delivery and improve health outcomes for millions of people living with diabetes in areas where dengue remains a major public health challenge.
View five news elements
Time
The review was published on 19 July 2026, and the findings were shared with the media on Sunday, with the paper presented at a convention in Kovalam.
Location and population
The review focuses on people with Type 2 diabetes in dengue-endemic regions, particularly in India and other areas where dengue is a significant public health challenge.
Event
A review paper by diabetes specialists found that people with Type 2 diabetes face a significantly higher risk of severe, life-threatening dengue complications. The paper recommends integrating dengue vaccination into routine diabetes care. [N2, N3, N4, N10, N12].
Scale, impact and risk
The review highlights that persistently high blood sugar impairs immune function and damages blood vessels, making people with diabetes more susceptible to severe dengue infection, including dengue haemorrhagic fever and dengue shock syndrome.
Response, status and uncertainty
The findings were shared with the media, and the paper was presented at the 14th Jothydev’s Professional Education Forum Annual Global Diabetes Convention. The review recommends integrating dengue vaccination into routine diabetes care to reduce severe illness and hospitalisation. [N5, N7, N10, N12, N13].

审查警告称,2 型糖尿病患者患登革热的风险更高;专家权衡

People with type 2 diabetes face higher risk of dengue, warns review; experts weigh in
新闻简报一篇即将发表在《国际糖尿病与技术杂志》上的评论论文警告说,由于免疫功能受损以及与持续高血糖相关的血管损伤,2 型糖尿病患者面临更高的严重登革热并发症风险,包括登革出血热和休克综合征。该审查由印度领先的糖尿病学家撰写并得到国际糖尿病联合会的认可,建议将登革热疫苗接种纳入流行地区的常规糖尿病护理中。专家指出,患有登革热的糖尿病患者更有可能经历严重的后果,尽管该审查承认这种关联并不能证明因果关系,并且肥胖或高血压等混杂因素可能会产生影响。该论文建议针对登革热症状尽早就医,经常监测血糖,并在患病期间临时​​调整药物。虽然审查呼吁将疫苗接种作为糖尿病管理的一部分,但专家强调建议必须符合国家政策。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026-07-21(2026年7月21日)。事件日期:所提供证据中未报告。综述论文计划发表在《国际糖尿病与技术杂志》(未指定事件时间)。
地点与对象
印度(国家背景)。受影响人群:2型糖尿病患者,特别是血糖持续高、血糖控制不佳或患有肥胖、肾病或高血压等合并症的患者。没有提及具体的社区或设施。
事件
一篇综述论文(非随机试验)警告说,由于免疫功能受损和血管损伤,2 型糖尿病患者面临严重登革热并发症的风险更高。该论文建议将登革热疫苗接种纳入登革热流行地区的常规糖尿病护理中。印度媒体援引专家评论报道了这一事件;这是对现有证据的审查,而不是已确认的疫情爆发。
规模、影响与风险
所提供的证据中未报告。该审查强调印度糖尿病患者严重感染和并发症的风险高出 2-4 倍,但没有提供病例数、死亡人数或暴露人数。据描述,由于过度的炎症反应(“细胞因子风暴”),严重登革热(例如登革出血热、登革休克综合征)的风险升高。
应对、状态与不确定性
该综述综合了现有的观察性研究和荟萃分析;它不构成积极的疫情应对措施。建议包括将登革热疫苗接种作为常规糖尿病护理的一部分,针对登革热症状进行早期医疗咨询,以及在登革热疾病期间调整糖尿病药物。不确定性包括缺乏因果关系证据(关联与因果关系)以及年龄、肥胖或血糖控制不佳等混杂因素。该文章包含有关依赖公共领域信息的免责声明,并建议咨询医疗保健从业者。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

People with type 2 diabetes face higher risk of dengue, warns review; experts weigh in

News Brief
A review paper slated for publication in the *International Journal of Diabetes & Technology* warns that people with type 2 diabetes face a higher risk of severe dengue complications, including dengue haemorrhagic fever and shock syndrome, due to impaired immune function and vascular damage linked to persistently high blood sugar. Authored by leading Indian diabetologists and endorsed by the International Diabetes Federation, the review recommends integrating dengue vaccination into routine diabetes care in endemic regions. Experts note that diabetics with dengue are more likely to experience severe outcomes, though the review acknowledges that association does not prove causation and that confounding factors like obesity or hypertension may contribute. The paper advises early medical consultation for dengue symptoms, frequent blood glucose monitoring, and temporary medication adjustments during illness. While the review calls for vaccination as part of diabetes management, experts stress that recommendations must align with national policies.
View five news elements
Time
Publication date: 2026-07-21 (Jul 21, 2026). Event date: Not reported in the supplied evidence. The review paper is slated for publication in the International Journal of Diabetes & Technology (event timing not specified).
Location and population
India (national context). Affected population: People with type 2 diabetes, particularly those with persistently high blood sugar, poor glycaemic control, or comorbidities such as obesity, kidney disease, or hypertension. No specific community or facility is mentioned.
Event
A review paper (not a randomized trial) warns that people with type 2 diabetes face a higher risk of severe dengue complications due to impaired immune function and vascular damage. The paper recommends integrating dengue vaccination into routine diabetes care in dengue-endemic regions. The event is reported by Indian media citing expert commentary; it is a review of existing evidence, not a confirmed outbreak.
Scale, impact and risk
Not reported in the supplied evidence. The review highlights a 2–4 times higher risk of severe infections and complications among diabetics in India, but no case counts, deaths, or exposure numbers are provided. The risk of severe dengue (e.g., dengue haemorrhagic fever, dengue shock syndrome) is described as elevated due to exaggerated inflammatory responses ('cytokine storm').
Response, status and uncertainty
The review is a synthesis of existing observational studies and meta-analyses; it does not constitute an active outbreak response. Recommendations include dengue vaccination as part of routine diabetes care, early medical consultation for dengue symptoms, and adjustments to diabetes medications during dengue illness. Uncertainties include the lack of causal proof (association vs. causation) and confounding factors such as age, obesity, or poor glycaemic control. The article includes disclaimers about relying on public domain information and advises consulting healthcare practitioners.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

印度首个获批登革热疫苗:武田 QDENGA 可预防所有四种病毒血清型

India’s first approved dengue vaccine: Takeda’s QDENGA protects against all four virus serotypes
新闻简报India’s Drug Controller General of India (DCGI) has granted market authorization for Takeda’s dengue vaccine QDENGA (TAK-003), marking the country’s first approved dengue vaccine. The two-dose vaccine, recommended for individuals aged 4–60 years, provides protection against all four dengue virus serotypes without requiring pre-vaccination screening. Approval follows robust clinical data from global and Indian Phase III trials, showing up to 80.2% efficacy against virologically confirmed dengue and 90.4% efficacy against hospitalization at 18 months. Takeda has partnered with Biological E to locally produce 50 million doses annually, supporting a global production target of 100 million doses per year by 2030. The move addresses India’s substantial dengue burden, which accounts for nearly one-third of global cases, with over 113,000 officially reported in 2025 and likely tens of millions of undetected infections.（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026 年 7 月 20 日。活动日期:印度药品监管局 (DCGI) 于 2026 年的某个未指定日期授予批准(N1、N4)。
地点与对象
印度(N1、N4、N31)。目标人群:4-60岁人群(N1、N4、N31)。
事件
DCGI 授予武田登革热疫苗 QDENGA (TAK-003) 市场授权,标志着印度首个获批的登革热疫苗(N1、N3、N4、N5、N31)。该批准基于证明安全性、免疫原性和有效性的全球和印度临床试验(N17、N18、N23)。
规模、影响与风险
印度占全球登革热负担的近三分之一(N10)。2025 年,官方报告了超过 113,000 例登革热病例,模型显示,由于漏报,每年有数千万例感染病例 (N11)。证据中没有说明具体的风险级别。该疫苗针对所有四种登革热病毒血清型(N14、N26)。
应对、状态与不确定性
QDENGA is approved for use in individuals aged 4–60 years without pre-vaccination testing (N1, N2, N4, N5, N31). The approval follows Phase III trials with efficacy up to 80.2% against virologically confirmed dengue and 90.4% against hospitalization at 18 months (N19, N20). Takeda has partnered with Biological E to manufacture 50 million doses annually in India, supporting a global production target of 100 million doses per year by the end of the decade (N32, N33). No unresolved information or limitations are reported in the supplied evidence.

News Brief
India’s Drug Controller General of India (DCGI) has granted market authorization for Takeda’s dengue vaccine QDENGA (TAK-003), marking the country’s first approved dengue vaccine. The two-dose vaccine, recommended for individuals aged 4–60 years, provides protection against all four dengue virus serotypes without requiring pre-vaccination screening. Approval follows robust clinical data from global and Indian Phase III trials, showing up to 80.2% efficacy against virologically confirmed dengue and 90.4% efficacy against hospitalization at 18 months. View five news elements
Time
Published on 20 July 2026. Event date: approval granted by the Drug Controller General of India (DCGI) on an unspecified date in 2026 (N1, N4).
Location and population
India (N1, N4, N31). Target population: individuals aged 4–60 years (N1, N4, N31).
Event
The DCGI granted market authorization for Takeda’s dengue vaccine QDENGA (TAK-003), marking India’s first approved dengue vaccine (N1, N3, N4, N5, N31). The approval is based on global and Indian clinical trials demonstrating safety, immunogenicity, and efficacy (N17, N18, N23).
Scale, impact and risk
India accounts for nearly one-third of the global dengue burden (N10). In 2025, more than 113,000 dengue cases were officially reported, with modelling suggesting tens of millions of infections annually due to underreporting (N11). No specific risk level is stated in the evidence. The vaccine targets all four dengue virus serotypes (N14, N26).
Response, status and uncertainty
QDENGA is approved for use in individuals aged 4–60 years without pre-vaccination testing (N1, N2, N4, N5, N31). The approval follows Phase III trials with efficacy up to 80.2% against virologically confirmed dengue and 90.4% against hospitalization at 18 months (N19, N20). Takeda has partnered with Biological E to manufacture 50 million doses annually in India, supporting a global production target of 100 million doses per year by the end of the decade (N32, N33). No unresolved information or limitations are reported in the supplied evidence.

新研究警告称,高血糖可能使登革热对 2 型糖尿病患者来说更加危险

High blood sugar could make Dengue more dangerous for Type 2 diabetes patients, warn new study
新闻简报一项新的研究警告说,由于持续的高血糖水平,2 型糖尿病患者患严重登革热的风险明显更高。研究表明,高血糖会削弱免疫系统并损害血管,增加登革热危及生命的并发症的可能性。News9live 报道的这项研究强调了控制血糖水平以改善登革热感染期间预后的重要性。这表明血糖控制良好的人出现严重并发症的可能性较小。这些发现强化了越来越多的证据表明 2 型糖尿病与严重登革热的风险较高有关,强调在蚊子季节需要保持警惕并尽早就医。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026-07-20。所提供的证据中未报告事件日期。
地点与对象
所提供的证据中未报告该位置。受影响人群是 2 型糖尿病患者。
事件
一项新的研究警告说,由于持续的高血糖水平,2 型糖尿病患者患严重登革热的风险明显更高。该研究强调,高血糖会削弱免疫系统并损害血管,增加危及生命的登革热并发症的风险。知名媒体 News9live 报道了此次活动。
规模、影响与风险
研究表明,血糖控制不佳的人更容易患上严重登革热。所提供的证据中没有报告病例、死亡或暴露的确切数量。明确指出,血糖水平控制不佳的人的风险较高。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究强调了控制血糖水平以改善登革热等传染病治疗结果的重要性。它还表明血糖水平控制良好的人出现严重并发症的可能性较小。目前的状况是,这些发现强化了越来越多的证据,表明 2 型糖尿病与严重登革热风险较高有关。来源是来自知名媒体的完整文章。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

High blood sugar could make Dengue more dangerous for Type 2 diabetes patients, warn new study

News Brief
A new study warns that people with Type 2 diabetes are at a significantly higher risk of developing severe dengue due to persistently high blood sugar levels. The research indicates that high blood sugar weakens the immune system and damages blood vessels, increasing the likelihood of life-threatening complications from dengue. The study, reported by News9live, highlights the importance of managing blood glucose levels to improve outcomes during dengue infections. It suggests that individuals with well-controlled blood sugar are less likely to experience severe complications. The findings reinforce the growing evidence linking Type 2 diabetes with a higher risk of severe dengue, emphasizing the need for vigilance and early medical attention during mosquito season.

The event is reported by News9live, a reputable media source.
Scale, impact and risk

The exact number of cases, deaths, or exposures is not reported in the supplied evidence. The risk is explicitly stated as higher for those with poorly controlled blood sugar levels.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study emphasizes the importance of managing blood glucose levels to improve outcomes during infectious diseases like dengue. It also suggests that people with well-controlled blood glucose levels are less likely to experience serious complications. The current status is that the findings reinforce growing evidence linking Type 2 diabetes with a higher risk of severe dengue. The source is a full article from a reputable media source.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

登革病毒3型(DENV-3)3III_B.3.2谱系在巴西的分子特征与近期传播:蛋白基序与流行病学的信息学(in silico)分析 - 生物通

登革病毒3型(DENV-3)3III_B.3.2谱系在巴西的分子特征与近期传播:蛋白基序与流行病学的信息学(in silico)分析 - 生物通
新闻简报发表在 Virus Genes 上的一项研究分析了巴西 3 型登革热病毒 (DENV-3) 谱系 3III_B.3.2 的分子特征和近期传播情况。该研究基于2013年至2025年的数据,显示DENV-3病例显着增加,特别是在2024年和2025年,其中巴西东南部地区受影响最严重。该研究鉴定了九种不同的蛋白质基序,并指出 3III_B.3.2 谱系已经取代了以前的循环谱系,表明这些变化可能有助于其复制成功。该研究还强调需要进一步进行体内研究,以了解不同血清型和基因型的特征,以及蛋白质基序对循环基因型的影响。研究结果强调了持续监测和研究对于预防和控制巴西 DENV-3 疫情的重要性。（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
The study analyzed data from 2013 to 2025, with a significant increase in DENV-3 cases noted in 2024 and 2025. The publication date is 2026-07-20.
地点与对象
该研究重点关注巴西,尤其是东南部地区,受影响最严重的年龄组是20-39岁,其次是40-59岁和15-19岁。
事件
该研究报告称,巴西的 DENV-3 病例显着增加,特别是 3III_B.3.2 谱系,与前几年相比,该谱系与更严重的临床结果和更高的病例数相关。该研究是对巴西 DENV-3 的分子表征和流行病学分析。
规模、影响与风险
2014年至2025年,巴西共记录了15,970,928例登革热确诊病例,仅2024年就有5,988,245例病例。DENV-3病例从2023年的106例显着增加到2024年的1,792例,到2025年12月达到6,766例。该研究鉴定了九种不同的蛋白质基序,其中特定磷酸化和糖基化位点显着增加。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究基于公共数据库的数据和计算分析。它强调需要进一步进行体内研究,以了解不同血清型和基因型的特征,以及蛋白质基序对循环基因型的影响。该研究还指出了依赖公开存放样本的局限性以及潜在的地理和时间抽样偏差。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

登革病毒3型(DENV-3)3III_B.3.2谱系在巴西的分子特征与近期传播:蛋白基序与流行病学的信息学(in silico)分析 - 生物通

News Brief
A study published in Virus Genes analyzed the molecular characteristics and recent spread of dengue virus type 3 (DENV-3) lineage 3III_B.3.2 in Brazil. The research, based on data from 2013 to 2025, revealed a significant increase in DENV-3 cases, particularly in 2024 and 2025, with the southeastern region of Brazil being the most affected. The study identified nine different protein motifs and noted that the 3III_B.3.2 lineage has replaced previous circulating lineages, suggesting that these changes may have contributed to its replication success. The study also highlighted the need for further in vivo research to understand the characteristics of different serotypes and genotypes, and the impact of protein motifs on circulating genotypes. The findings underscore the importance of continued surveillance and research to prevent and control DENV-3 outbreaks in Brazil.
View five news elements
Time
The study analyzed data from 2013 to 2025, with a significant increase in DENV-3 cases noted in 2024 and 2025. The publication date is 2026-07-20.
Location and population
The study focuses on Brazil, particularly the southeastern region, with the most affected age groups being 20–39 years, followed by 40–59 years and 15–19 years.
Event
The study reports a significant increase in DENV-3 cases in Brazil, particularly the 3III_B.3.2 lineage, which has been associated with more severe clinical outcomes and a higher number of cases compared to previous years. The study is a molecular characterization and epidemiological analysis of DENV-3 in Brazil.
Scale, impact and risk
From 2014 to 2025, Brazil recorded a total of 15,970,928 confirmed dengue cases, with 5,988,245 cases in 2024 alone. DENV-3 cases increased significantly from 106 in 2023 to 1,792 in 2024, and reached 6,766 by December 2025. The study identified nine different protein motifs, with notable increases in specific phosphorylation and glycosylation sites.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study is based on data from public databases and computational analysis. It highlights the need for further in vivo studies to understand the characteristics of different serotypes and genotypes, and the impact of protein motifs on circulating genotypes. The study also notes the limitations of relying on publicly deposited samples and the potential for geographic and temporal sampling biases.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

登革热疫情使孟加拉国麻疹危机应对变得复杂

Dengue outbreak complicates Bangladesh measles crisis response
新闻简报孟加拉国正在与登革热和麻疹的双重流行作斗争,这导致其卫生系统崩溃。截至 2026 年 7 月 6 日,该国今年已记录了 7,110 例登革热病例和 19 例死亡,而麻疹疫情已导致 113,000 多人住院,718 人死亡,其中大部分是五岁以下儿童。这些危机相互交织:登革热和麻疹有共同的症状,如发烧和皮疹,导致危险的误诊,延误适当的治疗。达卡的医院已经因麻疹死亡而不堪重负,由于实验室检测有限且没有针对这两种疾病的具体治疗方法,正在努力控制这两种疾病的爆发。专家警告说,即将到来的雨季(通常在八月和九月达到高峰)将加剧这两种病毒的传播,进一步加剧资源紧张。孟加拉国尚未获得登革热疫苗许可,有针对性的疫苗接种活动仍然不可行,导致医生没有工具来全面应对这两种流行病。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026-07-15。事件期间:2026年1月至5月(登革热),截至2026年7月6日(登革热),以及持续至2026年5月的麻疹流行(含报告的住院病例与死亡病例N1、N2、N3、N4)。
地点与对象
孟加拉国(全国),特别关注达卡及其医院(N1、N11、N​​22)。受影响人群包括5岁以下和10岁以下儿童(N4、N12)。
事件
孟加拉国正面临登革热疫情与麻疹流行的双重危机,该国医疗系统不堪重负。据《电讯报》报道,两种疾病症状重叠且资源紧张,形成双重危机(N1、N5、N7、N8、N9、N11、N22)。
规模、影响与风险
登革热:截至 2026 年 7 月 6 日(N3),已有 7,110 例病例和 19 例死亡。麻疹:超过 113,000 人住院治疗,718 人死亡,其中主要是五岁以下儿童 (N4)。由于重叠症状(发烧和皮疹)而导致误诊的风险,导致治疗不当和严重并发症(N7、N8、N9、N10、N18)。卫生系统面临压力,包括达卡医院在 12 小时内收治了多达 11 例麻疹死亡病例 (N11)。登革热优势菌株向 DENV2 的转变增加了严重继发感染 (N23) 的风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
医疗系统不堪重负;自 3 月中旬 (N22) 以来,达卡的 DNCC Covid-19 专用医院被改造成麻疹专科医院。存在实验室检测,但获取途径不完整,导致诊断复杂化 (N17)。对于这两种疾病都没有专门的治疗方法;孟加拉国尚未获得登革热疫苗(N37、N39)许可。专家强调了麻疹疫苗覆盖率跟踪和早期登革热病毒监测方面的差距(N35、N36)。雨季(8 月至 9 月为高峰)预计将加剧这两种疾病(N29、N30、N31、N32、N33)的传播。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Dengue outbreak complicates Bangladesh measles crisis response

News Brief
Bangladesh is battling a dual epidemic of dengue fever and measles that is pushing its health system to collapse. By July 6, 2026, the country had recorded 7,110 dengue cases and 19 deaths this year, while a measles outbreak has hospitalized over 113,000 people and killed 718, mostly children under five. The crises are compounding each other: dengue and measles share symptoms like fever and rash, leading to dangerous misdiagnoses that delay appropriate care. Hospitals in Dhaka, already overwhelmed by measles deaths, are struggling to manage both outbreaks amid limited laboratory testing and no specific treatments for either disease. Experts warn that the upcoming rainy season, which typically peaks in August and September, will intensify transmission of both viruses, further straining resources. Bangladesh has not licensed a dengue vaccine, and targeted vaccination campaigns remain unfeasible, leaving doctors without the tools to fully address either epidemic.
View five news elements
Time
Publication date: 2026-07-15. Event period: January to May 2026 (dengue), up to July 6, 2026 (dengue), and ongoing measles epidemic with hospitalizations and deaths reported through May 2026 (N1, N2, N3, N4).
Location and population
Bangladesh (nationwide), with specific focus on Dhaka and its hospitals (N1, N11, N22). Affected populations include children under five and under 10 years old (N4, N12).
Event
A concurrent dengue fever outbreak and measles epidemic are overwhelming Bangladesh's health system, reported by The Telegraph as a dual crisis with overlapping symptoms and resource strain (N1, N5, N7, N8, N9, N11, N22).
Scale, impact and risk
Dengue: 7,110 cases and 19 deaths by July 6, 2026 (N3). Measles: over 113,000 hospitalizations and 718 deaths, predominantly among children under five (N4). Risk of misdiagnosis due to overlapping symptoms (fever and rash), leading to inappropriate treatment and severe complications (N7, N8, N9, N10, N18). Health system strain includes Dhaka hospitals treating up to 11 measles deaths in 12 hours (N11). Dominant dengue strain shift to DENV2 increases risk of severe secondary infections (N23).
Response, status and uncertainty
Healthcare system overwhelmed; Dhaka's DNCC Dedicated Covid-19 Hospital repurposed as measles-only facility since mid-March (N22). Laboratory testing exists but access is patchy, complicating diagnosis (N17). No dedicated treatment for either disease; Bangladesh has not licensed a dengue vaccine (N37, N39). Experts highlight gaps in vaccine coverage tracking for measles and early dengue virus surveillance (N35, N36). Rainy season (peaking August-September) expected to intensify transmission of both diseases (N29, N30, N31, N32, N33).
原文 / Source

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

武田登革热疫苗 QDENGA (TAK-003) 获得 DCGI 批准 - Express Pharma

Takeda gets DCGI approval for its dengue vaccine QDENGA (TAK-003) - Express Pharma
新闻简报India’s drug regulator, the DCGI, has granted market authorization to Takeda’s dengue vaccine QDENGA (TAK-003) for individuals aged 4–60, marking the first dengue vaccine approved in the country. The two-dose, tetravalent live-attenuated vaccine targets all four dengue virus serotypes and can be used without pre-vaccination screening. Approval follows robust clinical data from over 28,000 participants across 19 trials, including the pivotal TIDES study, which demonstrated 80.2% efficacy against virologically confirmed dengue at 12 months and 90.4% protection against hospitalization at 18 months. QDENGA has already received WHO prequalification and is authorized in 43 countries with more than 32 million doses distributed globally. Takeda plans to collaborate with Indian authorities to support equitable access and broader dengue prevention efforts, including surveillance, vector control, and community engagement.（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间

规模、影响与风险
Not applicable (regulatory approval event, not an outbreak). Vaccine efficacy: 80.2% against virologically confirmed dengue at 12 months; 90.4% against dengue-related hospitalization at 18 months; 84.1% against dengue-related hospitalization after 4.5 years. WHO prequalification obtained; 43 countries approved; >32 million doses distributed globally.

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Takeda gets DCGI approval for its dengue vaccine QDENGA (TAK-003) - Express Pharma

News Brief
India’s drug regulator, the DCGI, has granted market authorization to Takeda’s dengue vaccine QDENGA (TAK-003) for individuals aged 4–60, marking the first dengue vaccine approved in the country. The two-dose, tetravalent live-attenuated vaccine targets all four dengue virus serotypes and can be used without pre-vaccination screening. Approval follows robust clinical data from over 28,000 participants across 19 trials, including the pivotal TIDES study, which demonstrated 80.2% efficacy against virologically confirmed dengue at 12 months and 90.4% protection against hospitalization at 18 months. QDENGA has already received WHO prequalification and is authorized in 43 countries with more than 32 million doses distributed globally. Takeda plans to collaborate with Indian authorities to support equitable access and broader dengue prevention efforts, including surveillance, vector control, and community engagement.
View five news elements
Time
Publication date: 20 July 2026; Event date: approval granted under NDCT Rules, 2019 (CT-20) on or before 20 July 2026.
Location and population
India (national); target population aged 4–60 years.
Event
Regulatory approval granted by the Drug Controller General of India (DCGI) for Takeda’s dengue vaccine QDENGA (TAK-003) under the New Drugs and Clinical Trials Rules, 2019.
Scale, impact and risk
Not applicable (regulatory approval event, not an outbreak). Vaccine efficacy: 80.2% against virologically confirmed dengue at 12 months; 90.4% against dengue-related hospitalization at 18 months; 84.1% against dengue-related hospitalization after 4.5 years. WHO prequalification obtained; 43 countries approved; >32 million doses distributed globally.
Response, status and uncertainty
Approval based on Phase I–III trials (>28,000 participants) including pivotal TIDES (DEN-301) trial (>20,000 participants). Indian Phase III trial (DEN-302) confirmed safety and immunogenicity in ages 4–60. Ongoing pharmacovigilance and real-world evidence generation planned. No unresolved safety signals reported in supplied evidence.

武田获得印度首个登革热疫苗 QDENGA 的 DCGI 认可 - Siliconindia

Takeda Secures DCGI Nod For India's First Dengue Vaccine QDENGA - Siliconindia
新闻简报日本武田生物制药公司于 2026 年 7 月 20 日宣布,印度药品监管总局 (DCGI) 已批准该国首个登革热疫苗 QDENGA。这种四价减毒活疫苗针对所有四种登革热血清型,并被授权用于 4-60 岁的个人,无需事先进行感染筛查,从而简化了常规护理中的接种。19 项试验中超过 28,000 名参与者的临床数据显示,两次给药方案一年后,对实验室确诊的登革热的疗效为 80.2%,18 个月后住院治疗的预防率为 90.4%,且持续获益长达七年。世卫组织已对 QDENGA 进行资格预审,并推荐将其用于登革热流行环境,支持更广泛的公共卫生用途。2025 年,印度记录了超过 113,000 例登革热病例,但模型显示真正的负担要高得多。武田现在计划与印度监管机构和卫生利益相关者合作,扩大获取范围,作为国家登革热预防战略的一部分。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026 年 7 月 20 日 14:26 IST(证据中未指定事件日期)。
地点与对象
印度(全国范围);批准用于 4-60 岁的个人。
事件
印度药品监管总局 (DCGI) 批准武田登革热疫苗 QDENGA,这是印度首个登革热疫苗授权。
规模、影响与风险
2025 年印度报告登革热病例将超过 113,000 例;由于漏报和无症状病例,每年真正的感染人数可能有数千万。没有证据表明具体的风险级别。
应对、状态与不确定性
疫苗获批有效期为 4-60 年,分两次接种,无需疫苗接种前筛查。武田计划与印度当局合作推出;世卫组织推荐将 QDENGA 用于流行病环境,并已对其进行资格预审。报告长达七年的长期安全性/有效性数据。未解决:印度批准后的实施细节和覆盖范围。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Takeda Secures DCGI Nod For India's First Dengue Vaccine QDENGA - Siliconindia

News Brief
Japan-based Takeda Biopharmaceuticals announced on 20 July 2026 that India’s Drug Controller General (DCGI) has granted approval for QDENGA, the country’s first dengue vaccine. The live-attenuated, tetravalent vaccine targets all four dengue serotypes and is authorized for individuals aged 4–60 without prior infection screening, simplifying delivery in routine care. Clinical data from over 28,000 participants across 19 trials show 80.2% efficacy against laboratory-confirmed dengue one year after the two-dose schedule, with 90.4% protection against hospitalizations after 18 months and sustained benefit up to seven years. WHO has prequalified QDENGA and recommends it for dengue-endemic settings, supporting broader public health use. India recorded more than 113,000 dengue cases in 2025, though modeling suggests the true burden is far higher. Takeda now plans to collaborate with Indian regulators and health stakeholders to scale access as part of the national dengue prevention strategy.
View five news elements
Time
Published 20 July 2026 at 14:26 IST (event date not specified in the evidence).
Location and population
India (national scope); approved for individuals aged 4–60 years.
Event
Regulatory approval granted by the Drug Controller General of India (DCGI) for Takeda’s dengue vaccine QDENGA, marking India’s first dengue vaccine authorization.
Scale, impact and risk
India reports over 113,000 dengue cases in 2025; true annual infections likely in the tens of millions due to underreporting and asymptomatic cases. No specific risk level stated in evidence.
Response, status and uncertainty
Vaccine approved for 4–60 years, two-dose schedule, no pre-vaccination screening required. Takeda plans rollout with Indian authorities; WHO recommends QDENGA for endemic settings and has prequalified it. Long-term safety/efficacy data up to seven years reported. Unresolved: post-approval implementation details and coverage scale in India.

印度批准首个登革热疫苗;这是您需要了解的内容 - Outlookbusiness.com

India Clears First Dengue Vaccine; Here's What You Need to Know - outlookbusiness.com
新闻简报India has approved Takeda's QDENGA, the first dengue vaccine cleared in the country, marking a significant milestone in combating one of the world's fastest-growing mosquito-borne diseases. The two-dose vaccine does not require pre-screening for prior dengue infection, addressing a key limitation of earlier vaccines like Sanofi's Dengvaxia. Globally, dengue cases surged to 14.4 million with over 11,000 deaths in 2024, with India alone recording 232,425 cases and 233 deaths. QDENGA demonstrated 90.4% efficacy in reducing dengue-related hospitalizations, offering hope in managing the disease's severity. However, experts caution that while the vaccine lowers hospitalization rates, it does not prevent outbreaks as long as mosquito transmission persists. Takeda's collaboration with Biological E will expand production in India, while other developers continue research, highlighting the ongoing challenges in dengue vaccine development.（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026-07-20。所提供的证据中未报告事件日期。
地点与对象
该疫苗也是与印度 Biological E 合作生产的。世界卫生组织报告的 2024 年全球登革热病例和死亡人数。
事件
印度药品监管机构批准了武田的 QDENGA,这是该国首个获得批准的登革热疫苗。该疫苗采用两剂方案,不需要对先前的登革热感染进行预筛查。路透社报道证实。
规模、影响与风险
Globally, 14.4 million dengue cases and 11,201 deaths in 2024. India recorded 232,425 cases and 233 deaths in 2024. QDENGA demonstrated 90.4% efficacy against dengue-related hospitalizations after 18 months.
应对、状态与不确定性
武田与 Biological E 合作扩大在印度的生产。监管机构可能已寻求额外的上市后安全数据。专家指出,疫苗可以减轻严重程度,但只要蚊子继续传播,就无法防止疫情爆发。印度免疫学有限公司等其他疫苗开发商正处于研究阶段。

India Clears First Dengue Vaccine; Here's What You Need to Know - outlookbusiness.com

News Brief
India has approved Takeda's QDENGA, the first dengue vaccine cleared in the country, marking a significant milestone in combating one of the world's fastest-growing mosquito-borne diseases. The two-dose vaccine does not require pre-screening for prior dengue infection, addressing a key limitation of earlier vaccines like Sanofi's Dengvaxia. Globally, dengue cases surged to 14.4 million with over 11,000 deaths in 2024, with India alone recording 232,425 cases and 233 deaths. QDENGA demonstrated 90.4% efficacy in reducing dengue-related hospitalizations, offering hope in managing the disease's severity. However, experts caution that while the vaccine lowers hospitalization rates, it does not prevent outbreaks as long as mosquito transmission persists. Takeda's collaboration with Biological E will expand production in India, while other developers continue research, highlighting the ongoing challenges in dengue vaccine development.
View five news elements
Time
Published on 2026-07-20. Event date not reported in the supplied evidence.
Location and population
The vaccine is also produced in collaboration with Biological E in India. Global dengue cases and deaths reported by WHO in 2024.
Event
India's drug regulator approved Takeda's QDENGA, the first dengue vaccine cleared in the country. The vaccine is a two-dose regimen and does not require pre-screening for prior dengue infection. Confirmed by Reuters report.
Scale, impact and risk
Globally, 14.4 million dengue cases and 11,201 deaths in 2024. India recorded 232,425 cases and 233 deaths in 2024. QDENGA demonstrated 90.4% efficacy against dengue-related hospitalizations after 18 months.
Response, status and uncertainty
Takeda partnered with Biological E to expand production in India. Regulators may have sought additional post-marketing safety data. Experts note the vaccine reduces severity but does not prevent outbreaks as long as mosquito transmission continues. Other vaccine developers, like Indian Immunologicals Ltd, are in the research phase.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

DCGI 批准后,印度批准首个登革热疫苗 QDENGA - 印度时报

India approves its first dengue vaccine QDENGA after DCGI clearance - The Times of India
新闻简报在获得印度药品监管总局 (DCGI) 的批准后,印度批准了其首款登革热疫苗 QDENGA,该疫苗由武田生物制药印度公司开发。该疫苗被授权用于 4 至 60 岁的个人,不需要对既往登革热感染进行疫苗接种前筛查,从而简化了部署。武田的全球临床计划涉及 19 项试验的 28,000 多名参与者,支持这一批准,七年数据表明对所有四种登革热病毒血清型具有持续保护作用。世界卫生组织 (WHO) 已对 QDENGA 进行了资格预审,并建议其在流行地区使用,其中包括印度,该国承担着全球登革热负担的近三分之一。该疫苗分两次注射,每次 0.5 毫升,间隔三个月,与病媒控制和监测一起被定位为印度登革热预防战略的重要补充。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026 年 7 月 20 日 13:01 IST(证据中未指定事件日期)。
地点与对象
印度(全国范围);批准用于 4 至 60 岁人群的疫苗。
事件
印度药品监管总局(DCGI)向武田登革热疫苗 QDENGA 授予市场授权,标志着该国首个登革热疫苗获得批准。
规模、影响与风险
所提供的证据中未报告。
应对、状态与不确定性
疫苗未经疫苗接种前筛选即可批准使用;注意到世卫组织资格预审;引用的长期七年保护数据;建议纳入更广泛的登革热预防战略。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

India approves its first dengue vaccine QDENGA after DCGI clearance - The Times of India

News Brief
India has approved its first dengue vaccine, QDENGA, developed by Takeda Biopharmaceuticals India, after receiving clearance from the Drug Controller General of India (DCGI). The vaccine is authorised for individuals aged 4 to 60 years and does not require pre-vaccination screening for prior dengue infection, simplifying deployment. Takeda’s global clinical programme, involving over 28,000 participants across 19 trials, supports the approval, with seven-year data indicating sustained protection against all four dengue virus serotypes. The World Health Organization (WHO) has prequalified QDENGA and recommends its use in endemic regions, including India, which bears nearly one-third of the global dengue burden. The vaccine is administered as two 0.5 ml injections three months apart and is positioned as a key addition to India’s dengue prevention strategy alongside vector control and surveillance.
View five news elements
Time
Published on 20 July 2026 at 13:01 IST (event date not specified in evidence).
Location and population
India (national scope); vaccine approved for individuals aged 4 to 60 years.
Event
India's Drug Controller General of India (DCGI) granted market authorisation to Takeda's dengue vaccine QDENGA, marking the country's first dengue vaccine approval.
Scale, impact and risk
Not reported in the supplied evidence.
Response, status and uncertainty
Vaccine approved for use without pre-vaccination screening; WHO prequalification noted; long-term seven-year protection data cited; integration into broader dengue prevention strategies recommended.

News Brief

The response focuses on targeted mosquito control, but the full extent of the dengue fever case and the effectiveness of these measures remain uncertain. This proactive step highlights the importance of public health measures in high-traffic areas like integrated resorts.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

日本公司的登革热疫苗成为第一个在印度获得监管部门批准的疫苗 - 印度时报

Japanese firm’s dengue vaccine becomes first to get regulatory approval in India - The Times of India
新闻简报India’s drug regulator has granted market authorization to QDENGA, a tetravalent live-attenuated dengue vaccine developed by Japan’s Takeda Biopharmaceuticals, making it the first dengue vaccine approved in the country. The two-dose subcutaneous regimen is indicated for individuals aged 4 to 60 years and can be administered regardless of prior dengue exposure, eliminating the need for pre-vaccination testing. Regulatory clearance follows earlier global approvals in 43 countries across Asia, Latin America, and Europe, with more than 32 million doses distributed worldwide. The move comes amid persistent dengue burden in India, where reported cases surpassed 113,000 in 2025 and modelling suggests the true incidence may be in the tens of millions annually. Details on India’s rollout plan, pricing, and post-marketing surveillance have not been disclosed.（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026 年 7 月 20 日,美国标准时间 17:02。事件日期:2026 年 7 月 20 日或之前获得监管部门批准(证据中未指定确切的事件日期)。
地点与对象
目标人群:4岁至60岁的人群。
事件
QDENGA 获得印度药品监管总局 (DCGI) 的监管批准,QDENGA 是武田生物制药公司开发的四价登革热减毒活疫苗,使其成为印度批准的首个登革热疫苗。状态:已确认官方监管行动。
规模、影响与风险
Not applicable to the approval event itself. Contextual burden: India accounts for nearly one-third of the global dengue burden; reported cases exceeded 113,000 in 2025, with modelling suggesting tens of millions of infections annually. Vaccine distribution: over 32 million doses distributed globally across 43 countries.
应对、状态与不确定性
获得监管部门批准;证据中没有提供进一步的调查或公共卫生应对细节。未解决:DCGI 批准的确切日期、实施时间表、采购计划和上市后监督要求。

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

印度获得第一种登革热疫苗——QDENGA - 德干先驱报

India gets its first Dengue vaccine—QDENGA - Deccan Herald
新闻简报India has approved its first dengue vaccine, QDENGA, developed by Japan's Takeda Pharmaceutical. This live-attenuated tetravalent vaccine protects against all four dengue virus serotypes and is administered as two subcutaneous injections three months apart to individuals aged 4 to 60, regardless of prior dengue exposure. The approval comes at a critical time, as dengue cases in India have increased 11-fold over the past two decades. QDENGA has been approved in 43 countries, with over 32 million doses distributed globally. Clinical trials have shown significant efficacy, with 61.2% effectiveness in preventing dengue after 4.5 years, increasing to 74.3% with a booster dose. The vaccine also demonstrated high efficacy against dengue-related hospitalizations, rising from 84.1% to 90.6% after a booster. This development adds an important preventive tool to India's public health arsenal, complementing existing measures like eliminating breeding sites and using mosquito repellents.（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
该文章发表于2026年7月20日。事件日期是印度中央药品标准控制部门(CDSCO)批准QDENGA疫苗的日期,虽然没有明确说明,但推测是在发表日期前后。该疫苗于 2022 年推出,此后已在 43 个国家获得批准 [N1、N2、N9]。
地点与对象
地点是印度,QDENGA疫苗已在印度获批使用。该疫苗适用于 4 至 60 岁的个体,无论之前是否接触过登革热[N4]。
事件
中央药品标准控制部(CDSCO)已批准日本武田制药的 QDENGA,使其成为印度首款登革热疫苗。QDENGA 是一种减毒活四价登革热疫苗,可预防所有四种登革热病毒血清型 [N2、N3]。
规模、影响与风险
The vaccine is administered as two subcutaneous injections three months apart. It has been approved in 43 countries, with more than 32 million doses distributed globally. The approval comes at a critical time for public health, as reported dengue cases in India have increased 11-fold in the past two decades [N1, N4, N5, N8].
应对、状态与不确定性
该疫苗的功效已在为期七年的 III 期 TIDES 临床试验中得到证实。试验数据显示,4.5年后,两剂QDENGA在预防登革热方面表现出61.2%的疫苗功效。4.5 岁时给予加强剂量,两年后疗效略有提高至 74.3%。该疫苗在 4.5 年时显示出对登革热相关住院治疗的有效性为 84.1%,在加强剂量后升至 90.6% [N20、N21、N22]。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

India gets its first Dengue vaccine—QDENGA - Deccan Herald

News Brief
India has approved its first dengue vaccine, QDENGA, developed by Japan's Takeda Pharmaceutical. This live-attenuated tetravalent vaccine protects against all four dengue virus serotypes and is administered as two subcutaneous injections three months apart to individuals aged 4 to 60, regardless of prior dengue exposure. The approval comes at a critical time, as dengue cases in India have increased 11-fold over the past two decades. QDENGA has been approved in 43 countries, with over 32 million doses distributed globally. Clinical trials have shown significant efficacy, with 61.2% effectiveness in preventing dengue after 4.5 years, increasing to 74.3% with a booster dose. The vaccine also demonstrated high efficacy against dengue-related hospitalizations, rising from 84.1% to 90.6% after a booster. This development adds an important preventive tool to India's public health arsenal, complementing existing measures like eliminating breeding sites and using mosquito repellents.
View five news elements
Time
The article was published on 20 July 2026. The event date is the approval of the QDENGA vaccine by the Central Drugs Standard Control Department (CDSCO) in India, which is not explicitly stated but inferred to be around the publication date. The vaccine was launched in 2022 and has been approved in 43 countries since then [N1, N2, N9].
Location and population
The location is India, where the QDENGA vaccine has been approved for use. The vaccine is administered to individuals aged 4 to 60, regardless of prior dengue exposure [N4].
Event
The Central Drugs Standard Control Department (CDSCO) has approved Japan's Takeda Pharmaceutical's QDENGA, making it India’s first dengue vaccine. QDENGA is a live-attenuated tetravalent dengue vaccine that protects against all four dengue virus serotypes [N2, N3].
Scale, impact and risk
The vaccine is administered as two subcutaneous injections three months apart. It has been approved in 43 countries, with more than 32 million doses distributed globally. The approval comes at a critical time for public health, as reported dengue cases in India have increased 11-fold in the past two decades [N1, N4, N5, N8].
Response, status and uncertainty
The vaccine's efficacy was demonstrated in a seven-year, Phase III TIDES clinical trial. Data from the trial shows that after 4.5 years, two doses of QDENGA demonstrated 61.2% vaccine efficacy in preventing dengue. A booster dose given at 4.5 years increased efficacy modestly to 74.3% after two years. The vaccine demonstrated 84.1% efficacy against dengue-related hospitalizations at 4.5 years, rising to 90.6% after the booster [N20, N21, N22].

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

QDENGA 的效果如何?印度批准日本制药商生产的首款针对 4 至 60 岁儿童的登革热疫苗 - theweek.in

How effective is QDENGA? India approves its first dengue vaccine by Japanese drugmaker for ages 4-60 - theweek.in
新闻简报India’s drug regulator has granted approval to Takeda’s QDENGA dengue vaccine, the first such authorization in the country, for use in individuals aged 4 to 60. The tetravalent live-attenuated vaccine, administered as two doses three months apart, showed 84% protection against dengue-related hospitalization and 61% protection against confirmed dengue in clinical trials involving nearly 28,000 participants over 4.5 years. The approval comes amid rising dengue burden in India, which accounts for roughly one-third of global cases, with 113,000 reported in 2025 and likely higher actual numbers. Takeda has partnered with Indian manufacturer Biological E to scale production to 50 million doses annually in India and 100 million globally by the end of the decade. The vaccine does not require pre-vaccination testing or prior infection assessment, simplifying deployment.（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
于 2026 年 7 月 20 日 14:53 IST 发布(未指定活动日期;发布前已采取批准行动)。
地点与对象
印度(国家批准);适用于 4 至 60 岁人群的疫苗。
事件
印度药品监管总局批准武田的 QDENGA 登革热疫苗用于 4-60 岁的个体,这是该国首个获得授权的登革热疫苗。
规模、影响与风险
印度占全球登革热感染病例的近三分之一;2025 年报告病例数达到 113,000 例(实际数字可能更高)。在为期 4.5 年、涉及约 28,000 名参与者的临床试验中,QDENGA 表现出对与登革热相关的住院治疗有 84% 的保护作用,对病毒学确诊的登革热有 61% 的保护作用。
应对、状态与不确定性
Vaccine can be administered regardless of prior dengue infection and without pre-vaccination testing. Takeda has partnered with Biological E to expand manufacturing in India, aiming for 50 million doses annually locally and 100 million globally by the end of the decade. Long-term protection data are reported as sustained, but country-specific post-marketing surveillance plans are not detailed in the evidence.

Location and population
India (national approval); vaccine intended for individuals aged 4 to 60 years.
Event
India’s Drug Controller General of India approved Takeda’s QDENGA dengue vaccine for use in individuals aged 4–60 years, marking the first dengue vaccine authorized in the country.

QDENGA demonstrated 84% protection against dengue-related hospitalization and 61% protection against virologically confirmed dengue overall in clinical trials involving ~28,000 participants over 4.5 years.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

自一月份以来登革热已造成 76 人死亡、13,500 人感染 - 亚洲新闻

Dengue kills 76, infects 13,500 people since January - AsiaNews
新闻简报自2010年1月以来,斯里兰卡已有13,500人感染登革热,并造成76人死亡,其中北部贾夫纳半岛由于积水和卫生条件差而面临尤其危险。流行病学家和医疗保健工会批评政府没有投资资源来阻止传播,特别是在雨季临近之际。卫生部被指责没有做好准备,尽管 BTI 防蚊用品获得批准,但尚未使用。由于缺乏疫苗以及维护清洁环境需要公众合作,这种情况更加恶化。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该事件发生在2010年1月至4月之间,文章发表于2010年5月13日[N3]。
地点与对象
疫情在斯里兰卡爆发,特别影响北部的贾夫纳半岛,那里的难民营积水和卫生条件差,增加了风险[N20]。
事件
自 2010 年 1 月以来,登革热已感染 13,500 人,并造成 76 人死亡。《亚洲新闻》援引流行病学家和医疗保健工会的话说,报道了这一事件 [N1,N4]。
规模、影响与风险
此次疫情已导致 13,500 人感染,76 人死亡。由于即将到来的雨季和政府未能实施预防措施,风险被明确指出为高[N4,N5]。
应对、状态与不确定性
流行病学家和医疗保健工会批评政府没有投资资源来阻止病毒传播。卫生部被指责没有做好准备,BTI 物资尽管获得了批准,但尚未使用[N5、N13、N14、N16、N17]。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Dengue kills 76, infects 13,500 people since January - AsiaNews

News Brief
Since January 2010, dengue fever has infected 13,500 people and caused 76 deaths in Sri Lanka, with the Jaffna Peninsula in the north being particularly at risk due to stagnant waters and poor sanitation. Epidemiologists and health care unions have criticized the government for not investing in resources to stop the spread, especially as the rainy season approaches. The Health Ministry is accused of being unprepared, and despite the approval of BTI supplies for mosquito control, they have not been used. The situation is exacerbated by the lack of a vaccine and the need for public cooperation in maintaining clean environments.

160 Dengue Cases In Baja California Sur: What Los Cabos Travelers Need To Know - Cabo Sun

News Brief
Health authorities in Baja California Sur have reported over 160 cases of dengue fever, prompting increased prevention measures and public awareness alerts. The illness, which is endemic to the region, is caused by the Aedes aegypti mosquito and is not a sudden outbreak but a persistent issue. Local government teams are actively working to contain the spread through fogging campaigns in residential and public areas. Travelers are advised to take basic precautions such as using bug spray containing DEET, especially during early morning and sunset hours when mosquitoes are most active. The region remains safe for travel, and there is no need to cancel vacations or change flight plans. Symptoms of dengue fever include high fever, severe headache, muscle and joint pain, and in rare cases, internal hemorrhaging. Most healthy travelers who contract the virus experience flu-like symptoms and recover with rest and hydration.

B routine priorityPublic-health news

High fever, body ache or chills? How to tell dengue from other monsoon fevers

News Brief
High fever, body ache or chills? How to tell dengue from other monsoon fevers Fever, body ache and headache are common during the monsoon, but not every fever is dengue. Knowing the differences between dengue, viral fever, malaria and typhoid can help you seek timely treatment and avoid complications. As the monsoon brings relief from the summer heat, it also marks the season when mosquito-borne and water-borne diseases begin to rise. Many people develop fever, body aches and headaches during this time, making it difficult to know whether they have a simple viral infection or something more serious like dengue, malaria or typhoid. The challenge is that these illnesses often begin with similar symptoms. In the first few days, almost all of them can cause fever, weakness and body pain.
View five news elements
Time
High fever, body ache or chills?
Location and population
Recognising these differences early can help people seek medical care at the right time and reduce the risk of serious complications.
Event
Many people develop fever, body aches and headaches during this time, making it difficult to know whether they have a simple viral infection or something more serious like dengue, malaria or typhoid.
Scale, impact and risk
Seek medical attention immediately if you have: - High fever above 102°F - Severe headache or pain behind the eyes - Persistent vomiting - Severe body aches - Rash - Bleeding from the gums or nose - Blood in vomit or stools - Extreme weakness or confusion - Difficulty drinking fluids Early diagnosis can help doctors monitor the illness closely and prevent severe dengue.
Response, status and uncertainty
If dengue is suspected, avoid taking these medicines unless advised by a doctor: - Aspirin - Ibuprofen - Diclofenac - Naproxen These drugs may: - Increase the risk of bleeding - Causes stomach irritation - Affects kidney function, especially if the patient is dehydrated Dr Tickoo also says there is no role for steroids or antibiotics in treating uncomplicated dengue because it is caused by a virus.

登革热会引发心脏问题吗?医生解释警告信号 - theweek.in

Can dengue trigger heart problems? Doctors explain the warning signs - theweek.in
新闻简报A new health explainer warns that while most dengue cases are mild, severe infections can trigger uncommon but life-threatening heart complications, including myocarditis and arrhythmias. Clinicians emphasize vigilance for persistent chest pain, unexplained breathlessness, palpitations, fainting, or low blood pressure that does not improve with standard dengue care. Evidence from a 2023 Taiwanese study of 65,906 confirmed dengue cases showed the highest risk of acute heart failure hospitalization within the first week of infection, particularly among adults over 60 and those hospitalized for dengue. Global dengue burden remains high, with 14.6 million cases reported worldwide in 2024 and over 4 million cases and 3,000 deaths recorded between January and July 2025 across 97 countries. Management focuses on early recognition and supportive care, as there is no specific antiviral treatment for dengue-related myocarditis.（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026 年 7 月 17 日 14:44 IST(未指定活动日期;指导文章)。
地点与对象
全球人口面临登革热风险;文章引用了印度和台湾;全世界的临床医生和患者。
事件
根据专家访谈和已发表的研究,解释报告的登革热感染心脏并发症(包括心肌炎)的指导文章。
规模、影响与风险
Global dengue burden: 100–400 million infections annually; 14.6 million cases in 2024; 4.1 million cases and 3,019 deaths reported to WHO from 97 countries between January–July 2025. Cardiac involvement is uncommon but recognized; risk higher in severe dengue and adults >60 years.
应对、状态与不确定性
文章提供了识别心脏警告信号(胸痛、呼吸困难、心悸、昏厥、持续低血压)和诊断检查(心电图、心脏生物标志物、超声心动图)的临床指导。没有针对登革热相关心肌炎的特异性抗病毒治疗;管理重点是水合和并发症治疗。来源限制:指导性文章,而非疫情报告。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Is It Just a Cold, Flu, or Dengue? A Parent’s Guide to Rainy Season Fevers

News Brief
As the rainy season sets in across the Philippines, parents often face anxiety over their children's health due to sudden fevers, coughs, and sniffles. The Little Zions Pediatrics Medical Group has published a guide to help parents differentiate between common colds, flu, and dengue fever. The guide explains that while fevers are a natural defense mechanism, recognizing the distinctive symptoms of each illness is crucial. For instance, the common cold symptoms develop slowly and include low-grade fever, runny nose, and sneezing, whereas the flu comes on suddenly with high fever, body pains, and exhaustion. Dengue fever, which rises during the rainy season due to mosquito breeding in standing water, presents with high fever, severe eye pains, muscle/joint pain, and red spots on the skin. The guide advises parents to monitor their child's fever closely and seek immediate medical attention if symptoms worsen or if the fever does not break by the end of the third day. Early medical checkups are recommended to identify complications and design exact treatment plans, ensuring the well-being of children during the rainy season.

Published on July 20, 2026, with the event date implied to be during the rainy season in the Philippines.

The Philippines, specifically targeting parents and children during the rainy season.

随着夏季旅行的增加,英国寨卡、登革热和疟疾病例增加 - 雅虎健康

Zika, dengue, and malaria cases rise in the UK as summer travel ramps up - Yahoo Health
新闻简报英国卫生官员报告称,随着夏季旅行的增加,寨卡病毒、登革热、基孔肯雅热和疟疾等蚊媒疾病有所增加。2026 年 1 月至 6 月期间,记录了 8 例寨卡病例,超过 2025 年的总数。登革热病例达到 137 例,基孔肯雅热感染病例总数为 59 例,疟疾病例为 557 例。其中 4 例寨卡病例与印度尼西亚有关,其他病例与泰国、马尔代夫和斯里兰卡有关。英国健康安全局 (UKHSA) 建议旅行者采取预防措施,包括使用驱虫剂、覆盖皮肤以及在经过处理的蚊帐下睡觉。特别警告孕妇注意寨卡病毒的风险。UKHSA 还建议审查官方旅行健康指南并服用必要的疫苗和抗疟疾药物。

时间
文章发表于2026-07-18。活动日期范围为 2026 年 1 月至 6 月。
地点与对象
受影响的地区包括英国,具体病例与印度尼西亚、泰国、马尔代夫和斯里兰卡有关。受影响的人群包括旅行者和孕妇。
事件
英国卫生官员报告称,寨卡病毒、登革热、基孔肯雅热和疟疾等蚊媒疾病有所增加。英国健康安全局(UKHSA)建议旅行者采取预防措施。该事件已通过报告的病例得到证实。
规模、影响与风险
2026 年 1 月至 6 月期间记录了 8 例寨卡病例,超过 2025 年报告的总数。输入性登革热病例 137 例,基孔肯雅热感染病例 59 例,疟疾病例 557 例。四起寨卡病例与印度尼西亚有关。明确指出了孕妇和旅行者的风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
UKHSA 建议旅行者查看官方旅行健康指南并采取预防措施,例如使用驱虫剂、覆盖皮肤以及在经过处理的蚊帐下睡觉。目前的状况正在接受监测,并正在传达预防建议。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Zika, dengue, and malaria cases rise in the UK as summer travel ramps up - Yahoo Health

News Brief
UK health officials have reported a rise in mosquito-borne diseases, including Zika, dengue, chikungunya, and malaria, as summer travel increases. Between January and June 2026, eight Zika cases were recorded, surpassing the total for 2025. Dengue cases reached 137, chikungunya infections totaled 59, and malaria cases were 557. Four Zika cases were linked to Indonesia, with other cases connected to Thailand, the Maldives, and Sri Lanka. The UK Health Security Agency (UKHSA) advises travelers to take precautions, including using repellent, covering skin, and sleeping under treated bed nets. Pregnant women are particularly warned about the risks of Zika. The UKHSA also recommends reviewing official travel health guidance and taking necessary vaccines and anti-malarial medication.
View five news elements
Time
The article was published on 2026-07-18. The event dates range from January to June 2026.
Location and population
The affected locations include the UK, with specific cases linked to Indonesia, Thailand, the Maldives, and Sri Lanka. The population affected includes travelers and pregnant women.
Event
UK health officials reported an increase in mosquito-borne diseases, including Zika, dengue, chikungunya, and malaria. The UK Health Security Agency (UKHSA) advised travelers to take precautions. The event is confirmed with reported cases.
Scale, impact and risk
Eight Zika cases were recorded between January and June 2026, exceeding the total reported in 2025. There were 137 imported dengue cases, 59 chikungunya infections, and 557 malaria cases. Four Zika cases were associated with Indonesia. The risk is explicitly stated for pregnant women and travelers.
Response, status and uncertainty
UKHSA is advising travelers to review official travel health guidance and take precautions such as using repellent, covering skin, and sleeping under treated bed nets. The current status is under monitoring and prevention advice is being communicated.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

登革热可能在 8 月至 9 月发生严重转变 - Prothom Alo

Dengue could take a severe turn in August-September - Prothom Alo English
新闻简报根据贾汉吉尔纳加尔大学昆虫饲养和实验站 (IRES) 进行的研究,预计孟加拉国的登革热将在 2026 年 8 月和 9 月特别严重。由于伊蚊通过公共交通工具的传播,这种疾病曾经仅限于达卡,现已传播到每个地区和乌帕齐拉城镇。高风险地区包括巴里沙尔、巴尔古纳、皮罗杰普尔、帕图卡利、巴格哈特、库尔纳、吉大港、科克斯巴扎尔、昌德普尔、库米拉、达卡、加济普尔、迈门辛、纳辛迪、马尼克甘吉、蒙希甘吉和纳拉扬甘吉。该文章强调需要针对伊蚊开展有针对性的蚊虫控制工作,因为目前的措施主要针对库蚊。伊蚊既可以在干净的水中繁殖,也可以在脏水中繁殖,其适应性构成了重大挑战。该文章强调了准备和预防措施的重要性,以缓解预计登革热病例激增的情况。
查看新闻五要素
时间
文章发表于2026-07-15。活动日期未指定,但预测适用于 2026 年 8 月和 9 月。
地点与对象
该活动涉及孟加拉国,特别是达卡和其他地区,包括巴里沙尔、巴尔古纳、皮罗杰普尔、帕图卡利、巴格哈特、库尔纳、吉大港、科克斯巴扎尔、昌德普尔、库米拉、加济布尔、迈门辛、纳尔辛迪、马尼克甘吉、蒙希甘吉和纳拉扬甘吉。受影响的人群包括居民和旅行者。
事件
本文讨论了孟加拉国登革热的传播和预测。据悉,登革热已成为全年流行的疾病,预计在2026年8月和2026年9月最为严重。该事件基于贾汉吉尔讷格尔大学昆虫饲养和实验站(IRES)进行的研究和监测。
规模、影响与风险
文章提到,预计上述地区的登革热病例将大幅增加。八月和九月风险尤其高。这种传播归因于伊蚊的适应性,它们可以在干净和肮脏的水中繁殖并与人一起旅行。
应对、状态与不确定性
该文章强调了各地区加强登革热防备的必要性。它还提到,目前的蚊子控制工作主要针对库蚊,而不是导致登革热的伊蚊。本文基于完整的原始报告。

Dengue could take a severe turn in August-September - Prothom Alo English

News Brief
Dengue in Bangladesh is forecasted to be particularly severe in August and September 2026, according to research conducted by the Insect Rearing and Experimental Station (IRES) of Jahangirnagar University. The disease, once confined to Dhaka, has spread to every district and upazila town due to the movement of Aedes mosquitoes via public transport. High-risk districts include Barishal, Barguna, Pirojpur, Patuakhali, Bagerhat, Khulna, Chattogram, Cox's Bazar, Chandpur, Cumilla, Dhaka, Gazipur, Mymensingh, Narsingdi, Manikganj, Munshiganj, and Narayanganj. The article emphasizes the need for targeted mosquito control efforts focusing on Aedes mosquitoes, as current measures primarily address Culex mosquitoes. The adaptability of Aedes mosquitoes, which can breed in both clean and dirty water, poses a significant challenge. The article underscores the importance of preparedness and preventive measures to mitigate the anticipated surge in dengue cases.
View five news elements
Time
The article was published on 2026-07-15. The event dates are not specified, but the forecast pertains to August and September 2026.
Location and population
The event pertains to Bangladesh, specifically Dhaka and other districts including Barishal, Barguna, Pirojpur, Patuakhali, Bagerhat, Khulna, Chattogram, Cox's Bazar, Chandpur, Cumilla, Gazipur, Mymensingh, Narsingdi, Manikganj, Munshiganj, and Narayanganj. The affected population includes residents and travelers.
Event
The article discusses the spread and forecast of dengue in Bangladesh. It is reported that dengue has become a year-round disease and is expected to be severe in August and September 2026. The event is based on research and surveillance conducted by the Insect Rearing and Experimental Station (IRES) of Jahangirnagar University.
Scale, impact and risk
The article mentions that dengue cases are expected to rise significantly in the mentioned districts. The risk is particularly high in August and September. The spread is attributed to the adaptability of Aedes mosquitoes, which can breed in both clean and dirty water and travel with people.
Response, status and uncertainty
The article highlights the need for stronger dengue preparedness across districts. It also mentions that current mosquito control efforts primarily target Culex mosquitoes, not Aedes mosquitoes, which are responsible for dengue. The article is based on a full original report.
